# L 開頭詞條

> 本檔收錄 3,758 條含中文對譯的詞目。

<a id="entry-l-000001"></a>
## l

*n.* 1. **公升, 升** — a metric unit of capacity, formerly defined as the volume of one kilogram of pure water under standard conditions; now equal to 1,000 cubic centimeters (or approximately 1.75 pints)
2. **五十** — the cardinal number that is the product of ten and five
3. **朗伯** — a cgs unit of illumination equal to the brightness of a perfectly diffusing surface that emits or reflects one lumen per square centimeter
4. the 12th letter of the Roman alphabet
*adj.* 1. **五十** — being ten more than forty

<a id="entry-l-000002"></a>
## l'aquila

*n.* 1. **拉魁拉, 鷲鳥座** — the provincial capital of the Abruzzi region in central Italy

<a id="entry-l-000003"></a>
## L'Hôpital's rule

**發音：** /ˌloʊpiːˈtɑːlz ˈɹuːl/
*prop. n.* 1. **洛必達法則 /洛必達法則, 羅必達法則 /羅必達法則** — The rule that the limit of the ratio of two functions equals the limit of the ratio of their derivatives, usable when the former limit is indeterminate and the latter limit exists.

<a id="entry-l-000004"></a>
## l-dopa

*n.* 1. **左旋多巴** — the levorotatory form of dopa (trade names Bendopa and Brocadopa and Larodopa); as a drug it is used to treat Parkinson's disease

<a id="entry-l-000005"></a>
## l-p

*n.* 1. **密紋唱機** — a long-playing phonograph record; designed to be played at 33.3 rpm

<a id="entry-l-000006"></a>
## L-plate

*n.* 1. **L牌** — A plastic square consisting of a large letter L, placed on the front and rear of a vehicle to indicate that the driver is a learner.

<a id="entry-l-000007"></a>
## l-plate

*n.* 1. **L字牌, L車牌, 學習者駕車牌照, 學員車牌** — a square plate bearing the letter L that is attached to both ends of a car to indicate that the driver is a learner

<a id="entry-l-000008"></a>
## l-shaped

*adj.* 1. **L形狀+的** — shaped in the form of the letter L

<a id="entry-l-000009"></a>
## la

*n.* 1. **鑭** — a white soft metallic element that tarnishes readily; occurs in rare earth minerals and is usually classified as a rare earth
2. a state in southern United States on the Gulf of Mexico; one of the Confederate states during the American Civil War
3. the syllable naming the sixth (submediant) note of a major or minor scale in solmization

<a id="entry-l-000010"></a>
## La Niña

**發音：** /lə ˈniːn.jə/
*prop. n.* 1. *(attributive, sometimes)* **拉尼娜, 拉尼娜現象 /拉尼娜現象** — A sustained negative sea-surface temperature anomaly of more than 0.5°C across the equatorial zone of the central and eastern Pacific Ocean; the negative phase of the multi-year ENSO cycle, which causes variations in local and regional weather patterns.

## lab coat

*n.* 1. **實驗外衣, 實驗室工作服, 實驗工作服** — a light coat worn to protect clothing from substances used while working in a laboratory

<a id="entry-l-000022"></a>
## lab rat

*n.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **白老鼠** — A person or group used as the subject of an experiment or test, especially unwillingly or unwittingly.

<a id="entry-l-000023"></a>
## label

**發音：** /ˈleɪ.bəl/
*v.* 1. assign a label to; designate with a label
*“These students were labelled `learning disabled'”*
2. attach a tag or label to
*“label these bottles”*
3. pronounce judgment on
*“They labeled him unfit to work here”*
4. distinguish (as a compound or molecule) by introducing a labeled atom
5. distinguish (an element or atom) by using a radioactive isotope or an isotope of unusual mass for tracing through chemical reactions
*n.* 1. **標記, 標記元素** — a radioactive isotope that is used in a compound in order to trace the mechanism of a chemical reaction
2. **標籤, 籤條** — an identifying or descriptive marker that is attached to an object
3. **標籤 /標籤, 簽條 /籤條** — A small ticket or sign giving information about something to which it is attached or intended to be attached.
4. a brief description given for purposes of identification
*“the label Modern is applied to many different kinds of architecture”*
5. trade name of a company that produces musical recordings
*“the artists and repertoire department of a recording label is responsible for finding new talent”*

<a id="entry-l-000024"></a>
## labellum

**發音：** /ləˈbɛl.əm/
*n.* 1. **唇瓣** — The lower central petal of a flower (especially an orchid), usually developed to be showy and attract pollinators.

<a id="entry-l-000025"></a>
## labeo

*n.* 1. **野鯪 /野鯪** — Any carp of the genus Labeo.

<a id="entry-l-000026"></a>
## labetalol

*n.* 1. **拉貝洛爾, 柳胺心定, 柳胺苄心定, 鹽酸拉貝洛爾** — antihypertensive drug (trade names Trandate and Normodyne) that blocks alpha and beta-adrenergic receptors of the sympathetic nervous system (leading to a decrease in blood pressure)

<a id="entry-l-000027"></a>
## labetalol hydrochloride

*n.* 1. **拉貝洛爾, 柳胺心定, 柳胺苄心定, 鹽酸拉貝洛爾** — antihypertensive drug (trade names Trandate and Normodyne) that blocks alpha and beta-adrenergic receptors of the sympathetic nervous system (leading to a decrease in blood pressure)

<a id="entry-l-000028"></a>
## labia

**發音：** /ˈleɪ.bi.ə/
*n.* 1. *(form-of, plural)* **陰唇 /陰唇** — plural of labium

<a id="entry-l-000029"></a>
## labia majora

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-normally)* **大陰唇 /大陰唇** — The two outer rounded folds of adipose tissue that lie on either side of the opening of the vagina.
2. the two outer folds of the vulva

<a id="entry-l-000030"></a>
## labia minora

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-normally)* **小陰唇 /小陰唇** — The two inner folds of skin within the cleft of the labia majora.
2. the two inner folds of the vulva

<a id="entry-l-000031"></a>
## labial

**發音：** /ˈleɪ.bi.əl/
*n.* 1. **唇音 /唇音** — A consonant articulated by the lips.
2. a consonant whose articulation involves movement of the lips
*adj.* 1. **嘴唇+的** — of or relating to the lips of the mouth
*“labial stops”*
2. **下唇瓣+的** — relating to or near the female labium
3. *(not-comparable)* **唇側 /唇側** — On the side facing the lips. See mesial.

<a id="entry-l-000032"></a>
## labial artery

*n.* 1. **唇動脈 /唇動脈** — Either of two branches of the facial artery of which one is distributed to the upper and one to the lower lip.
2. an artery that is a branch of the facial artery that supplies the lips of the mouth

<a id="entry-l-000033"></a>
## labial pipe

*n.* 1. **管風琴的唇管** — organ pipe whose tone is produced by air passing across the sharp edge of a fissure or lip

<a id="entry-l-000034"></a>
## labialization

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **唇音化** — A secondary articulatory feature of usually consonants that involves the contraction or rounding of the lips (labia) during pronunciation.

<a id="entry-l-000035"></a>
## labiaplasty

*n.* 1. **陰唇整型手術 /陰唇整型手術, 陰唇縮小手術 /陰唇縮小手術** — The moulding or shaping of the labia minora and/or labia majora.

<a id="entry-l-000036"></a>
## labiatae

*n.* 1. **唇形科** — a large family of aromatic herbs and shrubs having flowers resembling the lips of a mouth and four-lobed ovaries yielding four one-seeded nutlets and including mint; thyme; sage; rosemary

<a id="entry-l-000037"></a>
## labiodental

**發音：** /ˌleɪ.bi.əʊˈdɛn.təl/
*n.* 1. **唇齒音 /唇齒音** — A consonant articulated with the lower lip and upper teeth.
2. a consonant whose articulation involves the lips and teeth

<a id="entry-l-000038"></a>
## labium

**發音：** /ˈleɪ.bi.əm/
*n.* 1. *(plural-normally)* **唇 /唇** — A liplike structure; especially one of the vulva's two pairs of folds of skin on either side.
2. **唇 /唇** — The lip of a labiate corolla.
3. a liplike structure that bounds a bodily orifice (especially any of the four labiate folds of a woman's vulva)

<a id="entry-l-000039"></a>
## lablab

*n.* 1. **扁豆屬** — one species: hyacinth bean

<a id="entry-l-000040"></a>
## lablab purpureus

*n.* 1. **涼衍豆, 南扁豆, 南豆, 娥眉豆, 小刀豆** — perennial twining vine of Old World tropics having trifoliate leaves and racemes of fragrant purple pea-like flowers followed by maroon pods of edible seeds; grown as an ornamental and as a vegetable on the Indian subcontinent; sometimes placed in genus Dolichos

<a id="entry-l-000041"></a>
## labor

*n.* 1. **一夥, 一堆, 勞動階級, 勞工, 工人階級** — a social class comprising those who do manual labor or work for wages
*“there is a shortage of skilled labor in this field”*
2. **勞動, 勞工, 工作** — productive work (especially physical work done for wages)
*“his labor did not require a great deal of skill”*
3. **分娩** — concluding state of pregnancy; from the onset of contractions to the birth of a child
*“she was in labor for six hours”*
4. **任務, 項目, 事業, 工作, 方案** — any piece of work that is undertaken or attempted
*“he prepared for great undertakings”*
5. an organized attempt by workers to improve their status by united action (particularly via labor unions) or the leaders of this movement
6. a political party formed in Great Britain in 1900; characterized by the promotion of labor's interests and formerly the socialization of key industries
*v.* 1. **苦幹** — strive and make an effort to reach a goal
*“She tugged for years to make a decent living”*
*“We have to push a little to make the deadline!”*
2. **忙碌工作, 辛勞工作, 辛苦工作** — work hard
*“She was digging away at her math homework”*
*“Lexicographers drudge all day long”*
3. undergo the efforts of childbirth

<a id="entry-l-000042"></a>
## labor day

*n.* 1. **勞工節, 勞動節** — first Monday in September in the United States and Canada

<a id="entry-l-000043"></a>
## labor market

*n.* 1. **勞工市場 /勞工市場, 勞動市場 /勞動市場** — The market consisting of the supply of unemployed workers in relation to the demand for them.
2. the market in which workers compete for jobs and employers compete for workers

<a id="entry-l-000044"></a>
## labor of love

*n.* 1. *(US, idiomatic)* **義務勞動, 用愛發電** — A task performed voluntarily without expectation of reimbursement or practical benefit; an altruistic work or undertaking; an action or hobby performed purely out of an inner desire to do so.
2. **喜歡做的工作, 自願從事的工作** — productive work performed voluntarily without material reward or compensation

<a id="entry-l-000045"></a>
## labor pain

*n.* 1. **陣痛** — pain and discomfort associated with contractions of the uterus during labor

<a id="entry-l-000046"></a>
## labor pains

*n.* 1. **分娩時的陣痛** — a regularly recurrent spasm of pain that is characteristic of childbirth

<a id="entry-l-000047"></a>
## Labor Party

*prop. n.* 1. **工黨 /工黨** — Any of a number of political parties of various nationalities, often opposed to conservative parties.

<a id="entry-l-000048"></a>
## labor resources

*n.* 1. **人力資源, 勞動力資源** — resources of available manpower

<a id="entry-l-000049"></a>
## labor theory of value

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **勞動價值論 /勞動價值論** — The theory, usually associated with Marxian economics, that freely reproducible goods obtain their value from socially necessary human labor time.

<a id="entry-l-000050"></a>
## labor union

*n.* 1. **同盟, 工會, 工會聯合會, 協會** — an organization of employees formed to bargain with the employer
*“you have to join the union in order to get a job”*

<a id="entry-l-000051"></a>
## labor-intensive

*adj.* 1. *(US)* **勞動密集型 /勞動密集型** — Requiring a great deal of work, especially physical and manual effort versus capital.
2. requiring a large expenditure of labor but not much capital
*“cottage industries are labor intensive”*

<a id="entry-l-000052"></a>
## laboratory

**發音：** /ləˈbɒɹət(ə)ɹi/
*n.* 1. **實驗室, 研究室, 科研室** — a workplace for the conduct of scientific research
2. **研究室** — A place where chemicals, drugs or microbes are prepared or manufactured.
3. **實驗室 /實驗室** — A room, building or institution equipped for scientific research, experimentation or analysis.
4. a region resembling a laboratory inasmuch as it offers opportunities for observation and practice and experimentation
*“the new nation is a testing ground for socioeconomic theories”*
*“Pakistan is a laboratory for studying the use of American troops to combat terrorism”*

<a id="entry-l-000053"></a>
## laboratory bench

*n.* 1. **實驗臺, 實驗工作臺** — a workbench in a laboratory

<a id="entry-l-000054"></a>
## laboratory coat

*n.* 1. **實驗外衣, 實驗室工作服, 實驗工作服** — a light coat worn to protect clothing from substances used while working in a laboratory

<a id="entry-l-000055"></a>
## laboratory mouse

*n.* 1. **白鼠, 實驗鼠 /實驗鼠** — A small mammal of the order Rodentia, usually Mus musculus, which is bred and kept for scientific research.

<a id="entry-l-000056"></a>
## labored breathing

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **呼吸困難 /呼吸困難** — Breathing at less than 12 or more than 20 breaths per minute with poor or irregular quality, rhythm, or tidal volume; a sign of respiratory distress.

<a id="entry-l-000057"></a>
## laborer

**發音：** /ˈleɪ.bɚ.ɹɚ/
*n.* 1. **工人, 勞動者** — someone who works with their hands; someone engaged in manual labor
2. *(US)* **勞動者 /勞動者, 工人** — One who uses bodily strength rather than intellect to earn a wage, usually hourly.

<a id="entry-l-000058"></a>
## laborious

*adj.* 1. **艱苦+的, 費勁+的, 非常辛勞+的, 艱難+的, 費力+的** — characterized by effort to the point of exhaustion; especially physical effort
*“worked their arduous way up the mining valley”*
*“a grueling campaign”*

<a id="entry-l-000059"></a>
## laboriously

*adv.* 1. **不流暢+地, 費力+地, 辛苦+地** — in a laborious manner
*“their lives were spent in committee making decisions for others to execute on the basis of data laboriously gathered for them”*

<a id="entry-l-000060"></a>
## labour

**發音：** /ˈleɪ.bə/
*n.* 1. **一夥, 一堆, 勞動階級, 勞工, 工人階級** — a social class comprising those who do manual labor or work for wages
*“there is a shortage of skilled labor in this field”*
2. **勞動, 勞工, 工作** — productive work (especially physical work done for wages)
*“his labor did not require a great deal of skill”*
3. **分娩** — concluding state of pregnancy; from the onset of contractions to the birth of a child
*“she was in labor for six hours”*
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **生產 /生產** — The act or process of a mother giving birth.
5. a political party formed in Great Britain in 1900; characterized by the promotion of labor's interests and formerly the socialization of key industries
*v.* 1. *(intransitive)* **工作** — To toil, to work.
2. **忙碌工作, 辛勞工作, 辛苦工作** — work hard
*“She was digging away at her math homework”*
*“Lexicographers drudge all day long”*
3. **苦幹** — strive and make an effort to reach a goal
*“She tugged for years to make a decent living”*
*“We have to push a little to make the deadline!”*
4. undergo the efforts of childbirth

<a id="entry-l-000061"></a>
## labour aristocracy

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **工人貴族 /工人貴族, 勞工貴族 /勞工貴族** — Segment of the working class that enjoys a relatively privileged position compared to the rest of the working class as a result of higher salaries, better working conditions, etc., and which can lead them to have different interests and perspectives even though they continue to be exploited by capitalists.

<a id="entry-l-000062"></a>
## labour camp

*n.* 1. **勞改營 /勞改營** — A type of prison where prisoners are obliged to do forced labour, usually outdoors.
2. a penal institution for political prisoners who are used as forced labor

<a id="entry-l-000063"></a>
## labour force

*n.* 1. *(UK)* **勞動力 /勞動力** — The collective group of people who are available for employment, whether currently employed or unemployed (though sometimes only those unemployed people who are seeking work are included).

<a id="entry-l-000064"></a>
## labour law

*n.* 1. *(Canada, UK, uncountable)* **勞動法 /勞動法** — The body of statute law that regulates the relationship between employer, employee, labour union and government.

<a id="entry-l-000065"></a>
## labour of love

*n.* 1. **喜歡做的工作, 自願從事的工作** — productive work performed voluntarily without material reward or compensation

<a id="entry-l-000066"></a>
## labour pains

*n.* 1. **分娩時的陣痛** — a regularly recurrent spasm of pain that is characteristic of childbirth

<a id="entry-l-000067"></a>
## Labour Party

*prop. n.* 1. **工黨 /工黨** — Any of various political parties that aim to advance the interests of the working class, often characterised by left-of-centre politics and support for organised labour.

<a id="entry-l-000068"></a>
## labour power

*n.* 1. *(Marxism, uncountable)* **勞動力 /勞動力** — The capacity to do work (as opposed to labour, the physical act of working) which becomes a commodity under capitalism.

<a id="entry-l-000069"></a>
## labourer

*n.* 1. **工人, 勞動者** — someone who works with their hands; someone engaged in manual labor

*prop. n.* 1. **納閩 /納閩** — A federal territory off Sabah, Malaysia.

## laburnum

**發音：** /ləˈbɝnəm/
*n.* 1. **毒豆, 金鏈花 /金鍊花, 金急雨, 臘腸樹 /臘腸樹** — Any tree of the genus Laburnum.

<a id="entry-l-000076"></a>

*n.* 1. **寒荷, 旱地蓮, 旱蓮, 旱蓮花, 金梅草** — an ornamental shrub or tree of the genus Laburnum; often cultivated for Easter decorations

<a id="entry-l-000078"></a>
## labyrinth

**發音：** /ˈlæb.ə.ɹɪnθ/
*n.* 1. **迷宮** — complex system of paths or tunnels in which it is easy to get lost
2. *(Greek)* **明陣 /明陣, 迷宮 /迷宮** — A complicated irregular network of passages or paths, especially underground or covered, in which it is difficult to find one's way.
3. a complex system of interconnecting cavities; concerned with hearing and equilibrium

<a id="entry-l-000079"></a>
## labyrinth of minos

*n.* 1. **米諾斯迷宮** — a vast labyrinth built in Crete by Daedalus at the command of Minos in order to contain the Minotaur

<a id="entry-l-000080"></a>
## labyrinthian

*adj.* 1. **迷宮+的, 迷陣似+的, 錯綜複雜+的** — resembling a labyrinth in form or complexity
*“a labyrinthine network of tortuous footpaths”*

<a id="entry-l-000081"></a>
## labyrinthine

**發音：** /ˌlab.əˈɹɪn.θʌɪn/
*adj.* 1. **內耳+的, 迷路+的, 迷宮+的** — relating to or affecting or originating in the inner ear
*“labyrinthine deafness”*
2. **迷路的** — Relating to the labyrinth of the inner ear.
3. **迷宮+的, 迷陣似+的, 錯綜複雜+的** — resembling a labyrinth in form or complexity
*“a labyrinthine network of tortuous footpaths”*

<a id="entry-l-000082"></a>
## labyrinthine artery

*n.* 1. **迷路動脈 /迷路動脈** — A long slender artery that arises from the basilar artery or one of its branches, accompanies the vestibulocochlear nerve through the internal auditory canal, and is distributed to the inner ear.
2. an artery that is a branch of the basilar artery that supplies the labyrinth

<a id="entry-l-000083"></a>
## labyrinthitis

*n.* 1. **內耳炎, 迷路炎** — inflammation of the inner ear; can cause vertigo and vomiting

## lac

<a id="entry-l-000086"></a>
## lace

**發音：** /leɪs/
*v.* 1. *(ergative, transitive)* **繫 /系** — To fasten (something) with laces.
2. spin,wind, or twist together
*“intertwine the ribbons”*
*“Twine the threads into a rope”*
3. make by braiding or interlacing
*“lace a tablecloth”*
4. do lacework
*“The Flemish women were lacing in front of the cathedral”*
5. draw through eyes or holes
*“lace the shoelaces”*
6. add alcohol to (beverages)
*“the punch is spiked!”*
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **花邊 /花邊** — A light fabric containing patterns of holes, usually built up from a single thread.
2. **繫帶, 鞋帶, 飾帶, 花邊, 帶子** — a cord that is drawn through eyelets or around hooks in order to draw together two edges (as of a shoe or garment)
3. **蕾絲, 透孔織品, 網眼織物** — a delicate decorative fabric woven in an open web of symmetrical patterns

<a id="entry-l-000087"></a>

**發音：** /ˈleɪ.di/
*n.* 1. **小姐, 夫人** — An aristocratic title for a woman.

<a id="entry-l-000168"></a>
## lady

**發音：** /ˈleɪ.di/
*n.* 1. **女士, 貴婦** — a polite name for any woman
*“a nice lady at the library helped me”*
2. **女士, 貴婦 /貴婦** — A woman of breeding or higher class, a woman of authority.
3. *(historical)* **女主人** — The mistress of a household.
4. *(in-plural)* **女廁所 /女廁所** — A polite reference or form of address to women.
5. a woman of refinement
*“a chauffeur opened the door of the limousine for the grand lady”*
6. a woman of the peerage in Britain

*n.* 1. **瓢蟲** — small round bright-colored and spotted beetle that usually feeds on aphids and other insect pests

## lag

**發音：** /læɡ/
*v.* 1. **落後** — hang (back) or fall (behind) in movement, progress, development, etc.
2. **落後 /落後, 跟不上** — To fail to keep up (the pace), to fall behind.
3. **下獄, 關押, 囚禁, 幽閉, 監禁** — lock up or confine, in or as in a jail
*“The suspects were imprisoned without trial”*
*“the murderer was incarcerated for the rest of his life”*
4. *(informal)* **卡頓 /卡頓** — To respond slowly.
5. throw or pitch at a mark, as with coins
6. cover with lagging to prevent heat loss
*“lag pipes”*
*n.* 1. *(countable)* **落差, 延遲 /延遲** — A gap, a delay; an interval created by something not keeping up; a latency.
2. **過渡期間** — the time between one event, process, or period and another
*“meanwhile the socialists are running the government”*
3. *(Ireland, UK, countable)* **囚犯, 罪犯** — A prisoner, a criminal.
4. the act of slowing down or falling behind
5. one of several thin slats of wood forming the sides of a barrel or bucket
*adj.* 1. **晚** — Late.
2. *(obsolete)* **最後 /最後** — Last; long-delayed.

<a id="entry-l-000369"></a>
## land

**發音：** /lænd/
*n.* 1. **不動產** — the land on which real estate is located
*“he built the house on land leased from the city”*
2. **土地, 地, 地面, 陸地** — the solid part of the earth's surface
*“the plane turned away from the sea and moved back over land”*
*“the earth shook for several minutes”*
3. **王國, 領域** — a domain in which something is dominant
*“the untroubled kingdom of reason”*
*“a land of make-believe”*
4. **不動產** — extensive landed property (especially in the country) retained by the owner for his own use
*“the family owned a large estate on Long Island”*
5. **國, 國土, 國家, 州, 共和國** — a politically organized body of people under a single government
*“the state has elected a new president”*
*“African nations”*
6. *(countable, uncountable)* **土地, 陸地 /陸地** — The part of Earth which is not covered by oceans or other bodies of water.
*v.* 1. **著陸, 降落** — reach or come to rest
*“The bird landed on the highest branch”*
*“The plane landed in Istanbul”*
2. **降落** — cause to come to the ground
*“the pilot managed to land the airplane safely”*
3. **使上岸, 使登陸** — bring ashore
*“The drug smugglers landed the heroin on the beach of the island”*
4. *(intransitive)* **著地, 著地, 著陸 /著陸, 著陸** — To descend to a surface, especially from the air.
5. **登岸, 靠岸** — arrive on shore
*“The ship landed in Pearl Harbor”*
6. **擊落, 打下** — shoot at and force to come down
*“the enemy landed several of our aircraft”*

<a id="entry-l-000370"></a>
## land bridge

**發音：** /ˈlænd ˌbɹɪd͡ʒ/
*n.* 1. **陸橋 /陸橋** — An isthmus or other land connection between landmasses, chiefly one which existed during prehistoric times, by which it is theorized that animals and plants were able to spread from one landmass to another.
2. **陸運走廊 /陸運走廊** — A relatively fast rail or road route over land, especially one for transporting goods.

<a id="entry-l-000438"></a>
## language

*n.* 1. **語言** — a systematic means of communicating by the use of sounds or conventional symbols
*“he taught foreign languages”*
*“the language introduced is standard throughout the text”*
2. **用語, 術語** — a system of words used to name things in a particular discipline
*“legal terminology”*
*“biological nomenclature”*
3. (language) communication by word of mouth
*“his speech was garbled”*
*“he uttered harsh language”*
4. the text of a popular song or musical-comedy number
*“his compositions always started with the lyrics”*
*“he wrote both words and music”*
5. the cognitive processes involved in producing and understanding linguistic communication
*“he didn't have the language to express his feelings”*
6. the mental faculty or power of vocal communication
*“language sets homo sapiens apart from all other animals”*

<a id="entry-l-000439"></a>
## language arts

2. barrier to communication resulting from speaking different languages

<a id="entry-l-000442"></a>

<a id="entry-l-000443"></a>

<a id="entry-l-000446"></a>

<a id="entry-l-000447"></a>

*adj.* 1. **無精打採的 /無精打采的** — Of a person or their actions, character, etc.: lacking drive, emotion, or enthusiasm; apathetic, listless, spiritless, unenthusiastic.
2. lacking spirit or liveliness
*“a lackadaisical attempt”*
*“a languid mood”*

<a id="entry-l-000456"></a>
## languish

**發音：** /ˈlæŋ.ɡwɪʃ/
*v.* 1. **失去活力, 憔悴, 衰弱** — lose vigor, health, or flesh, as through grief
*“After her husband died, she just pined away”*
2. *(intransitive)* **憔悴** — To lose strength and become weak; to be in a state of weakness or sickness.
3. **想念, 渴望** — have a desire for something or someone who is not present
*“She ached for a cigarette”*
*“I am pining for my lover”*
4. become feeble
*“The prisoner has be languishing for years in the dungeon”*

<a id="entry-l-000457"></a>

## lap

**發音：** /læp/
*n.* 1. **腰以下及大腿的前面部分, 膝部** — the upper side of the thighs of a seated person
*“he picked up the little girl and plopped her down in his lap”*
2. **膝部** — The upper legs of a seated person.
3. **範圍** — an area of control or responsibility
*“the job fell right in my lap”*
4. **下襬** — the part of a piece of clothing that covers the thighs
*“his lap was covered with food stains”*
5. **重疊** — a flap that lies over another part
*“the lap of the shingles should be at least ten inches”*
6. **圈** — One circuit around a race track.
*v.* 1. **摺疊, 部分重疊** — lie partly over or alongside of something or of one another
2. **舐, 舔, 舔舐** — pass the tongue over
*“the dog licked her hand”*
3. *(ambitransitive)* **啜** — To take (liquid) into the mouth with the tongue; to lick up with a quick motion of the tongue.
4. **作嗖嗖聲, 發嗖嗖聲, 嘩嘩響** — move with or cause to move with a whistling or hissing sound
*“The bubbles swoshed around in the glass”*
*“The curtain swooshed open”*
5. take up with the tongue
*“The cat lapped up the milk”*
*“the cub licked the milk from its mother's breast”*
6. wash or flow against
*“the waves laved the shore”*

4. **消逝, 過去, 逝去, 流逝, 過** — pass by

## last

**發音：** /lɑːst/
*adj.* 1. **最後+的, 最後, 終點+的, 最終+的, 末端+的** — occurring at or forming an end or termination
*“his concluding words came as a surprise”*
*“the final chapter”*
2. **臨終, 最後+的** — occurring at the time of death
*“his last words”*
*“the last rites”*
3. *(not-comparable)* **最後 /最後, 最終 /最終** — Final, ultimate, coming after all others of its kind.
4. *(not-comparable)* **最近的, 上個 /上個** — Most recent, latest, last so far.
5. **不可更改+的, 最後+的, 確定性+的, 決定性+的, 最終+的** — not to be altered or undone
*“the judge's decision is final”*
*“the arbiter will have the last say”*
6. immediately past
*“last Thursday”*
*“the last chapter we read”*
*v.* 1. **持久, 持續, 連續, 延續** — persist for a specified period of time
*“The bad weather lasted for three days”*
2. **過著, 忍耐, 忍受, 持續** — continue to live through hardship or adversity
*“We went without water and food for 3 days”*
*“These superstitions survive in the backwaters of America”*
3. *(intransitive)* **延續 /延續, 持續 /持續** — To endure, continue over time.
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable, sequence)* **最後 /最後, 最終 /最終** — after everything else; finally
2. most_recently
*“I saw him last in London”*
3. the item at the end
*“last, I'll discuss family values”*
*n.* 1. **完結, 最後階段, 終止, 結束** — the temporal end; the concluding time
*“the stopping point of each round was signaled by a bell”*
*“the market was up at the finish”*
2. **最後** — the last or lowest in an ordering or series
*“he was the last to leave”*
*“he finished an inglorious last”*
3. **拉** — a unit of weight equal to 4,000 pounds
4. **拉** — a unit of capacity for grain equal to 80 bushels
5. **鞋楦** — holding device shaped like a human foot that is used to fashion or repair shoes
6. a person's dying act; the final thing a person can do
*“he breathed his last”*

<a id="entry-l-000647"></a>

*“last not least he plays the saxophone”*

## late

**發音：** /leɪt/
*adv.* 1. **晚, 遲 /遲, 遲到 /遲到** — After a deadline has passed, past a designated time.
2. later than usual or than expected
*“the train arrived late”*
*“we awoke late”*
3. to an advanced time
*“deep into the night”*
*“talked late into the evening”*
4. at an advanced age or stage
*“she married late”*
*“undertook the project late in her career”*
5. in the recent past
*“he was in Paris recently”*
*“lately the rules have been enforced”*
*adj.* 1. **誤期+的, 遲+的, 遲來+的** — after the expected or usual time; delayed
*“a belated birthday card”*
*“I'm late for the plane”*
2. **晚** — Near the end of a period of time.
3. *(euphemistic, not-comparable)* **已故** — Recently deceased, dead: used particularly when speaking of the dead person's actions while alive. (Generally must be preceded by a possessive or an article, commonly “the”; see usage notes. Can itself only precede the person's name, never follow it.)
4. being or occurring at an advanced period of time or after a usual or expected time
*“late evening”*
*“late 18th century”*
5. of the immediate past or just previous to the present time
*“a late development”*
*“their late quarrel”*
6. having died recently
*“her late husband”*

**發音：** /lɑːɹə/
*prop. n.* 1. **羅娜 /羅娜, 蘿拉 /蘿拉, 勞拉 /勞拉** — A female given name from Latin.

<a id="entry-l-000801"></a>
## Laurasia

*prop. n.* 1. **勞亞大陸 /勞亞大陸** — A supercontinent which included most of the landmasses making up today's continents of the Northern Hemisphere.

<a id="entry-l-000802"></a>
## laureate

**發音：** /ˈlɒɹ.i.ət/
*n.* 1. *(dated)* **獲獎者 /獲獎者** — One crowned with laurel, such as a poet laureate or Nobel laureate.
2. someone honored for great achievements; figuratively someone crowned with a laurel wreath
*adj.* 1. worthy of the greatest honor or distinction
*“The nation's pediatrician laureate is preparing to lay down his black bag"- James Traub”*

<a id="entry-l-000803"></a>
## laurel

**發音：** /ˈlɒɹ.əl/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **月桂** — An evergreen shrub of species Laurus nobilis, having aromatic leaves of a lanceolate shape, with clusters of small, yellowish white flowers in their axils.
2. **月桂冠, 桂冠** — (antiquity) a wreath of laurel foliage worn on the head as an emblem of victory
3. any of various aromatic trees of the laurel family
4. United States slapstick comedian (born in England) who played the scatterbrained and often tearful member of the Laurel and Hardy duo who made many films (1890-1965)

<a id="entry-l-000804"></a>
## laurel cherry

*n.* 1. **桂櫻** — frequently cultivated Eurasian evergreen shrub or small tree having showy clusters of white flowers and glossy foliage and yielding oil similar to bitter almond oil
2. **月桂, 月桂樹** — small flowering evergreen tree of southern United States

<a id="entry-l-000805"></a>
## laurel willow

*n.* 1. **五蕊柳** — European willow tree with shining leathery leaves; widely naturalized in the eastern United States

<a id="entry-l-000806"></a>
## laurel wreath

*n.* 1. **月桂冠** — A wreath, traditionally made of laurel, worn on the head as a symbol of victory.
2. **月桂冠, 桂冠** — (antiquity) a wreath of laurel foliage worn on the head as an emblem of victory
3. **獎章, 紀念章** — an award for winning a championship or commemorating some other event

<a id="entry-l-000807"></a>
## laurels

*n.* 1. **榮譽, 名譽** — the state of being honored
2. a tangible symbol signifying approval or distinction
*“an award for bravery”*

**發音：** /ˈlɒ.ɹəns/
*prop. n.* 1. **勞倫斯 /勞倫斯, 老楞佐** — A male given name from Latin, feminine equivalent Lauren.

<a id="entry-l-000809"></a>
## lauric acid

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **月桂酸** — A saturated fatty acid, CH₃(CH₂)₁₀COOH, occurring in coconut, palm and laurel oil; mostly used in making cosmetics and soaps
2. a crystalline fatty acid occurring as glycerides in natural fats and oils (especially coconut oil and palm-kernel oil)

<a id="entry-l-000810"></a>
## Lausanne

**發音：** /ləʊˈzæn/

<a id="entry-l-000811"></a>
## lava

**發音：** /ˈlɑːvə/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **熔岩** — The molten rock ejected by a volcano from a vent such as a crater or fissure; magma that has breached the surface of the earth.
2. rock that in its molten form (as magma) issues from volcanos; lava is what magma is called when it reaches the surface

<a id="entry-l-000812"></a>
## lava dome

*n.* 1. **火山穹丘** — A convex volcanic structure made of generally viscous, felsic lava, which grows slowly over time by extrusion from a volcanic edifice, either endogenously, by inflating from within, or exogenously, as erupted flow lobes of lava.

<a id="entry-l-000813"></a>
## lava lake

*n.* 1. **熔岩湖** — A reservoir of molten lava contained in a volcanic vent, crater, or broad depression.

<a id="entry-l-000814"></a>
## lava tube

*n.* 1. **熔岩管** — A natural conduit formed by flowing lava that moves beneath the hardened surface of a lava flow.

## lavash

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **拉瓦什** — A soft, thin flatbread made with flour, water, yeast, and salt, baked in a tandoor, and sometimes sprinkled with sesame seeds or poppy seeds before baking.

<a id="entry-l-000824"></a>
## lavatory

*n.* 1. **洗臉盆, 盥洗室, 臉盆** — a bathroom sink that is permanently installed and connected to a water supply and drainpipe; where you can wash your hands and face
*“he ran some water in the basin and splashed it on his face”*
2. **抽水馬桶** — a toilet that is cleaned of waste by the flow of water through it
3. a room or building equipped with one or more toilets

<a id="entry-l-000825"></a>
## lave

*v.* 1. **洗滌, 洗清, 洗澡, 洗臉, 洗** — cleanse (one's body) with soap and water
2. wash or flow against
*“the waves laved the shore”*
3. wash one's face and hands
*“She freshened up in the bathroom”*

<a id="entry-l-000826"></a>
## lavender

**發音：** /ˈlæv.ən.dəː/
*adj.* 1. **淺紫色的, 淡紫色+的** — of a pale purple color
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **薰衣草** — Any of a group of European plants, genus, Lavandula, of the mint family.
2. **靈香草, 薰衣草, 香水植物, 黃香草, 香草** — any of various Old World aromatic shrubs or subshrubs with usually mauve or blue flowers; widely cultivated
3. a pale purple color

<a id="entry-l-000827"></a>
## laver

*n.* 1. **洗禮盆** — (Old Testament) large basin used by a priest in an ancient Jewish temple to perform ritual ablutions
2. Australian tennis player who in 1962 was the second man to win the Australian and French and English and United States singles titles in the same year; in 1969 he repeated this feat (born in 1938)
3. edible red seaweeds
4. seaweed with edible translucent crinkly green fronds

<a id="entry-l-000828"></a>
## lavish

*v.* 1. **濫花, 浪費, 揮霍, 濫用** — expend profusely; also used with abstract nouns
*“He was showered with praise”*
*adj.* 1. **豐富+的, 奢華+的, 豪華+的, 浪費+的** — characterized by extravagance and profusion
*“a lavish buffet”*
*“a lucullan feast”*

*“distributed gifts with a lavish hand”*
*“the critics were lavish in their praise”*

## Lavrov

<a id="entry-l-000830"></a>
## law

*n.* 1. **法律系統** — the collection of rules imposed by authority
*“civilization presupposes respect for the law”*
*“the great problem for jurisprudence to allow freedom while enforcing order”*
2. **自然法, 自然法則** — a rule or body of rules of conduct inherent in human nature and essential to or binding upon human society
3. **公安, 警, 警方, 警察** — the force of policemen and officers
*“the law came looking for him”*
4. legal document setting forth rules governing a particular kind of activity
*“there is a law against kidnapping”*
5. a generalization that describes recurring facts or events in nature
*“the laws of thermodynamics”*
6. the branch of philosophy concerned with the law and the principles that lead courts to make the decisions they do

<a id="entry-l-000831"></a>
## law and order

**發音：** /lɔ.ən(d)ˈoɹdəɹ/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **法律和秩序, 法治, 治安, 公安** — The strict enforcement of law, statutes, and social conventions.

<a id="entry-l-000832"></a>
## law enforcement

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **執法 /執法** — The task of ensuring obedience to the law.
2. *(uncountable)* **執法部門 /執法部門** — The various government agencies, regarded collectively, involved in the prevention of crime and the apprehension of criminals.
3. ensuring obedience to the laws

<a id="entry-l-000833"></a>
## law firm

*n.* 1. **法律事務所 /法律事務所, 律師事務所 /律師事務所, 律所** — A business formed by one or more lawyers to engage in the practice of law.
2. a firm of lawyers

<a id="entry-l-000834"></a>
## law of archimedes

*n.* 1. **阿基米德原理** — (hydrostatics) the apparent loss in weight of a body immersed in a fluid is equal to the weight of the displaced fluid

<a id="entry-l-000835"></a>
## law of conservation of mass

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **質量守恆定律 /質量守恆定律** — Synonym of conservation of mass.
2. a fundamental principle of classical physics that matter cannot be created or destroyed in an isolated system

<a id="entry-l-000836"></a>
## law of cosines

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **餘弦定理 /餘弦定理** — A generalization of the Pythagorean theorem that relates the lengths of the sides of a triangle to the cosine of one of its angles.

<a id="entry-l-000837"></a>
## law of excluded middle

*n.* 1. **排中律** — A logical principle which states all statements must be either true or false, i.e. in symbols: P∨¬P.

<a id="entry-l-000838"></a>
## law of nations

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **國際法 /國際法** — Synonym of international law.
2. the body of laws governing relations between nations

<a id="entry-l-000839"></a>
## law of sines

*n.* 1. **正弦定理** — A statement that relates the lengths of the sides of a triangle to the sines of its angles.

<a id="entry-l-000840"></a>
## law of the jungle

*n.* 1. *(figuratively)* **叢林法則 /叢林法則, 弱肉強食 /弱肉強食** — A putative law dictating that one serves one's own interest to the extent that one can, in any situation where legal authority is absent or generally ignored; self-interested behaviour that emerges in the absence of law; lawlessness.

<a id="entry-l-000841"></a>
## law of the land

*n.* 1. **國法 /國法** — A particular law or the complete set of laws currently in effect within a jurisdiction, especially with emphasis on the official and authoritative nature of such law.
2. a phrase used in the Magna Carta to refer to the then established law of the kingdom (as distinct from Roman or civil law); today it refers to fundamental principles of justice commensurate with due process
*“the United States Constitution declares itself to be `the supreme law of the land'”*

<a id="entry-l-000842"></a>
## law of the sea

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **海洋法** — The body of international law that concerns the principles and rules by which public entities, especially states, interact in maritime matters, including navigational rights, sea mineral rights, and coastal waters jurisdiction.

<a id="entry-l-000843"></a>
## law school

*n.* 1. **法學院 /法學院** — An academic institution at which post-graduate students are prepared for the practice of law.
2. a graduate school offering study leading to a law degree

<a id="entry-l-000844"></a>
## law-abiding

*adj.* 1. **守法的** — Obeying the laws of society; not a lawbreaker; without a criminal record.
2. (of individuals) adhering strictly to laws and rules and customs
*“law-abiding citizens”*
*“observant of the speed limit”*

<a id="entry-l-000845"></a>
## law-breaking

*n.* 1. **刑事犯罪, 罪行, 罪, 犯罪** — (criminal law) an act punishable by law; usually considered an evil act
*“a long record of crimes”*

<a id="entry-l-000846"></a>
## law-makers

*n.* 1. **立法機關** — persons who make or amend or repeal laws

<a id="entry-l-000847"></a>
## lawcourt

*n.* 1. **法庭** — a tribunal that is presided over by a magistrate or by one or more judges who administer justice according to the laws

<a id="entry-l-000848"></a>
## lawfare

**發音：** /ˈlɔːfɛə(ɹ)/
*n.* 1. *(informal, uncountable)* **法律戰 /法律戰** — The bringing of legal proceedings against an opponent, often only to attack, harass, or intimidate.

<a id="entry-l-000849"></a>
## lawful

**發音：** /ˈlɔːfl̩/
*adj.* 1. **合法的** — Conforming to, or recognised by the laws of society.
2. **依法的, 受規律制約的, 有法可依的, 有規則可循的, 正當的** — according to custom or rule or natural law
3. conformable to or allowed by law
*“lawful methods of dissent”*
4. having a legally established claim
*“the legitimate heir”*
*“the true and lawful king”*
5. authorized, sanctioned by, or in accordance with law
*“a legitimate government”*

<a id="entry-l-000850"></a>
## lawfully

*adv.* 1. **合理+地, 正當+地, 合法+地** — in a manner acceptable to common custom
*“you cannot do this legitimately!”*
2. by law; conforming to the law
*“we are lawfully wedded now”*

<a id="entry-l-000851"></a>
## lawgiver

**發音：** /ˈlɔːˌɡɪv.ə/
*n.* 1. **立法者** — One who provides laws to a society.
2. a maker of laws; someone who gives a code of laws

<a id="entry-l-000852"></a>
## lawless

**發音：** /ˈlɔːləs/
*adj.* 1. **無政府的, 無法+的, 無秩序的** — without law or control
*“the system is economically inefficient and politically anarchic”*
2. **不受法律管轄的 /不受法律管轄的, 無法統制的 /無法統制的** — Not governed by any law.
3. **違法的 /違法的, 非法的** — Prohibited by law; unlawful, illegal.
4. **不受法律約束的 /不受法律約束的, 無紀律的 /無紀律的, 混亂的 /混亂的** — Not restrained by the law or by discipline; disorderly, unruly.
5. lax in enforcing laws
*“a wide-open town”*
6. disobedient to or defiant of law
*“lawless bands roaming the plains”*

<a id="entry-l-000853"></a>
## lawlessness

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **無法無天 /無法無天** — A lack of law; a lack of law and order; anarchy.
2. **無政府狀態, 無法無天, 無秩序, 混亂** — a state of lawlessness and disorder (usually resulting from a failure of government)
3. illegality as a consequence of unlawful acts; defiance of the law

<a id="entry-l-000854"></a>
## lawmaking

*n.* 1. **制定法律, 立法** — the act of making or enacting laws

## lawrencium

**發音：** /ləˈɹɛnsiəm/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **鐒 /鐒** — A transuranic chemical element (symbol Lr, formerly Lw) with atomic number 103.
2. **鐒** — a radioactive transuranic element synthesized from californium

<a id="entry-l-000864"></a>
## lawsuit

**發音：** /ˈlɔːˌs(j)uːt/
*n.* 1. **訴訟 /訴訟, 官司** — In civil law, a case where two or more people disagree and one or more of the parties take the case to a court for resolution.

<a id="entry-l-000867"></a>
## lax

*adj.* 1. **鬆元音+的, 松馳+的** — pronounced with muscles of the tongue and jaw relatively relaxed (e.g., the vowel sound in `bet')
2. **松+的** — lacking in strength or firmness or resilience
*“a lax rope”*
*“a limp handshake”*
3. lacking in rigor or strictness
*“such lax and slipshod ways are no longer acceptable”*
*“lax in attending classes”*
4. emptying easily or excessively
*“loose bowels”*

<a id="entry-l-000868"></a>
## laxation

*n.* 1. **拉屎, 排便, 排糞, 通便** — the elimination of fecal waste through the anus
2. the act of making something less tight

<a id="entry-l-000869"></a>
## laxative

**發音：** /ˈlæk.sə.tɪv/
*n.* 1. **輕瀉劑 /輕瀉劑, 瀉藥 /瀉藥, 通洩藥, 瀉劑 /瀉劑** — Any substance, such as a food or in the form of a medicine which has a laxative effect.
2. **瀉藥, 輕瀉劑, 輕瀉藥, 通便劑, 通洩藥** — a mild cathartic
*adj.* 1. **致洩的** — Having the effect of moving the bowels, or aiding digestion and preventing constipation.
2. stimulating evacuation of feces

<a id="entry-l-000870"></a>
## laxly

*“he felt incensed that Tarrant should have been treated so leniently given his crime”*

<a id="entry-l-000871"></a>
## lay

**發音：** /leɪ/
*v.* 1. **安置, 擱, 擺, 放, 置** — put into a certain place or abstract location
*“Put your things here”*
*“Set the tray down”*
2. **擱放, 置, 放, 放置** — put in a horizontal position
*“lay the books on the table”*
*“lay the patient carefully onto the bed”*
3. *(transitive)* **放, 置, 放置** — To place down in a position of rest, or in a horizontal position.
4. prepare or position for action or operation
*“lay a fire”*
*“lay the foundation for a new health care plan”*
5. lay eggs
*“This hen doesn't lay”*
6. impose as a duty, burden, or punishment
*“lay a responsibility on someone”*
*adj.* 1. **俗人+的, 世俗+的** — characteristic of those who are not members of the clergy
*“set his collar in laic rather than clerical position”*
*“the lay ministry”*
2. not of or from a profession
*“a lay opinion as to the cause of the disease”*
*n.* 1. a narrative song with a recurrent refrain
2. a narrative poem of popular origin

<a id="entry-l-000872"></a>
## lay an egg

*v.* 1. **產卵 /產卵** — To produce an egg.

<a id="entry-l-000873"></a>

*“He saves half his salary”*

## lay away

<a id="entry-l-000875"></a>
## lay claim

*“He claimed his suitcases at the airline counter”*
*“Mr. Smith claims special tax exemptions because he is a foreign resident”*

<a id="entry-l-000876"></a>
## lay eyes on

*v.* 1. **注意到, 看到, 見到, 觀察到** — see with attention
*“behold Christ!”*

<a id="entry-l-000877"></a>
## lay figure

*n.* 1. **人體活動模型** — dummy in the form of an artist's jointed model of the human body

<a id="entry-l-000878"></a>
## lay in

*v.* 1. **儲存, 貯藏, 貯存, 積蓄** — keep or lay aside for future use
*“store grain for the winter”*
*“The bear stores fat for the period of hibernation when he doesn't eat”*

<a id="entry-l-000879"></a>
## lay judge

*n.* 1. **國民法官 /國民法官, 人民陪審員 /人民陪審員** — A lay person who acts as (or is) a judge, or who assists a (professional) judge, in a court proceeding (especially in jurisdictions which do not use juries).

<a id="entry-l-000880"></a>
## lay of the land

*n.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **地形, 地勢 /地勢** — The physical characteristics of the terrain or surrounding natural environment.
2. *(idiomatic)* **形勢 /形勢** — The trends, feelings, intentions, and other factors influencing a strategic, political, or social situation.

<a id="entry-l-000881"></a>
## lay off

*v.* 1. **停, 使中止, 停止, 中止** — put an end to a state or an activity
*“Quit teasing your little brother”*
2. **休假, 放假, 暫時解僱** — dismiss, usually for economic reasons

<a id="entry-l-000883"></a>
## lay out

*v.* 1. **有準備的** — get ready for a particular purpose or event
*“set up an experiment”*
*“set the table”*

*“lay out the clothes”*
*“lay out the arguments”*
3. spend or invest
*“lay out thousands on gold”*
*“he laid out a fortune in the hope of making a huge profit”*

*“She laid out her plans for the new house”*

<a id="entry-l-000928"></a>
## lead

**發音：** /ˈlɛd/
*v.* 1. **引路, 引導, 帶領** — take somebody somewhere
*“We lead him to our chief”*
*“can you take me to the main entrance?”*
2. **導致, 致使, 留** — have as a result or residue
*“The water left a mark on the silk dress”*
*“Her blood left a stain on the napkin”*
3. **致使, 誘使** — tend to or result in
*“This remark lead to further arguments among the guests”*
4. **領路, 帶領** — travel in front of; go in advance of others
*“The procession was headed by John”*
5. **延伸, 達到** — stretch out over a distance, space, time, or scope; run or extend between two points or beyond a certain point
*“Service runs all the way to Cranbury”*
*“His knowledge doesn't go very far”*
6. **帶領, 率領, 主導, 引導** — be in charge of
*“Who is heading this project?”*
*n.* 1. **領先** — an advantage held by a competitor in a race
*“he took the lead at the last turn”*
2. **鉛** — a soft heavy toxic malleable metallic element; bluish white when freshly cut but tarnishes readily to dull grey
*“the children were playing with lead soldiers”*
3. **循路而行, 線索, 追蹤** — evidence pointing to a possible solution
*“the police are following a promising lead”*
*“the trail led straight to the perpetrator”*
4. **領導地位, 命令, 指導, 領導** — a position of leadership (especially in the phrase `take the lead')
*“he takes the lead in any group”*
*“we were just waiting for someone to take the lead”*
5. **主角, 瞄準的角** — the angle between the direction a gun is aimed and the position of a moving target (correcting for the flight time of the missile)
6. *(uncountable)* **鉛 /鉛** — A heavy, pliable, inelastic metal element, having a bright, bluish color, but easily tarnished; both malleable and ductile, though with little tenacity. It is easily fusible, forms alloys with other metals, and is an ingredient of solder and type metal. Atomic number 82, symbol Pb (from Latin plumbum).

The predecessor of the United Nations.

## leak

**發音：** /liːk/
*n.* 1. **漏洞** — A crack, crevice, fissure, or hole which admits water or other fluid, or lets it escape.
2. **洩漏 /洩漏** — The entrance or escape of a fluid through a crack, fissure, or other aperture.
3. **透露, 洩漏 /洩漏, 洩 /洩** — A divulgation, or disclosure, of information previously held secret.
4. **排洩** — a euphemism for urination
*“he had to take a leak”*
5. an accidental hole that allows something (fluid or light etc.) to enter or escape
*“one of the tires developed a leak”*
6. soft watery rot in fruits and vegetables caused by fungi
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **洩漏 /洩漏, 漏** — To allow fluid or gas to pass through an opening that should be sealed.
2. *(ambitransitive)* **漏** — To disclose secret information surreptitiously or anonymously.
3. **漏, 漏出, 漏洞，漏隙, 洩漏** — enter or escape as through a hole or crack or fissure
*“Water leaked out of the can into the backpack”*
*“Gas leaked into the basement”*
4. tell anonymously
*“The news were leaked to the paper”*
5. be leaked
*“The news leaked out despite his secrecy”*
6. have an opening that allows light or substances to enter or go out
*“The container leaked gasoline”*
*“the roof leaks badly”*

<a id="entry-l-001007"></a>
## leak fungus

*n.* 1. **匍枝根黴** — fungus causing soft watery rot in fruits and vegetables and rings of dry rot around roots of sweet potatoes

<a id="entry-l-001008"></a>
## leakage

**發音：** /ˈliːkɪd͡ʒ/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **洩漏 /洩漏** — An act of leaking, or something that leaks.
2. the discharge of a fluid from some container
*“they tried to stop the escape of gas from the damaged pipe”*
*“he had to clean up the leak”*

<a id="entry-l-001009"></a>
## lean

**發音：** /ˈliːn/
*v.* 1. **傾向, 傾斜, 屈身** — to incline or bend from a vertical position
*“She leaned over the banister”*
2. **易於, 趨向, 傾向, 走向** — have a tendency or disposition to do or be something; be inclined
*“She tends to be nervous before her lectures”*
*“These dresses run small”*
3. *(intransitive)* **傾斜 /傾斜** — To incline, deviate, or bend, from a vertical position; to be in a position thus inclining or deviating.
4. **倚靠, 隑 /隑, 倚** — To rest or rely, for support, comfort, to use as a hard surface for writing, etc.
5. **倚, 倚著 /倚著** — To press against.
6. **使傾斜** — cause to lean to the side
*“Erosion listed the old tree”*
*adj.* 1. **瘦+的** — lacking excess flesh
*“you can't be too rich or too thin”*
*“Yon Cassius has a lean and hungry look"-Shakespeare”*
2. **瘦, 精瘦** — Slim; not fleshy.
3. **貧瘠+的** — lacking in mineral content or combustible material
*“lean ore”*
*“lean fuel”*
4. **不夠+的, 貧瘠+的, 不足+的** — containing little excess
*“a lean budget”*
*“a skimpy allowance”*
5. **精益** — Efficient, economic, frugal, agile, slimmed-down; pertaining to the modern industrial principles of "lean manufacturing".
6. not profitable or prosperous
*“a lean year”*
*n.* 1. **傾向, 傾斜** — the property possessed by a line or surface that departs from the vertical
*“the tower had a pronounced tilt”*
*“the ship developed a list to starboard”*

<a id="entry-l-001010"></a>

*v.* 1. **仰靠著, 向後仰, 向後傾斜, 向後靠** — move the upper body backwards and down

2. an inclination to do something

## leap

**發音：** /ˈliːp/
*v.* 1. **彈跳, 跳, 跳動, 跳越, 跳過** — move forward by leaps and bounds
*“The horse bounded across the meadow”*
*“The child leapt across the puddle”*
2. *(intransitive)* **跳躍 /跳躍, 飛躍 /飛躍** — To jump.
3. **使躍過, 使跳起, 使跳躍** — cause to jump or leap
*“the trainer jumped the tiger through the hoop”*
4. pass abruptly from one state or topic to another
*“leap into fame”*
*“jump to a conclusion”*
5. jump down from an elevated point
*“the parachutist didn't want to jump”*
*“every year, hundreds of people jump off the Golden Gate bridge”*
*n.* 1. **上漲, 突升, 飛躍** — a sudden and decisive increase
*“a jump in attendance”*
2. a light, self-propelled movement upwards or forwards
3. an abrupt transition
*“a successful leap from college to the major leagues”*
4. the distance leaped (or to be leaped)
*“a leap of 10 feet”*

<a id="entry-l-001017"></a>
## leap day

**發音：** /liːp deɪ/
*n.* 1. **閏日 /閏日** — An extra day intercalated into a year, especially the day intercalated into the Julian calendar every fourth year and into the Gregorian calendar every fourth year excepting centuries not divisible by 400, usually reckoned as February 29th.
2. the name of the day that is added during a leap year

<a id="entry-l-001018"></a>
## leap of faith

2. **冒出, 躍出, 跳出來, 突然出現** — jump out from a hiding place and surprise (someone)
*“The attackers leapt out from the bushes”*

<a id="entry-l-001020"></a>
## leap second

*n.* 1. **閏秒 /閏秒** — A second of time added to the year occasionally to compensate for variation in the rate of Earth's rotation relative to the absolute standards of time.
2. **閏秒** — a second (as measured by an atomic clock) added to or subtracted from Greenwich Mean Time in order to compensate for slowing in the Earth's rotation

<a id="entry-l-001021"></a>
## leap year

**發音：** /ˈliːp ˌjɪə/
*n.* 1. **閏年 /閏年** — A year in the Julian or Gregorian calendar with an intercalary day added to February (in the Gregorian calendar, February 29), used to adjust for the extra hours of the solar year; a 366-day year.
2. **閏年** — in the Gregorian calendar: any year divisible by 4 except centenary years divisible by 400

<a id="entry-l-001022"></a>
## leapfrog

**發音：** /ˈliːpfɹɒɡ/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **蛙跳** — A game, often played by children, in which a player leaps like a frog over the back of another person who has stooped over. One variation of the game involves a number of people lining up in a row and bending over. The last person in the line then vaults forward over each of the others until they reach the front of the line, whereupon they also bend over. The process is then repeated.
2. advancing as if in the child's game, by leaping over obstacles or competitors
*“the company still believes the chip is a leapfrog in integration and will pay huge dividends”*
3. a game in which one child bends down and another leaps over
*v.* 1. **越級提升, 跳升, 躍過** — jump across
*“He leapfrogged his classmates”*
2. progress by large jumps instead of small increments

<a id="entry-l-001023"></a>

## lease

**發音：** /liːs/
*v.* 1. **租, 出租, 租出** — let for money
*“We rented our apartment to friends while we were abroad”*
2. **租, 租用, 租出, 出租** — hold under a lease or rental agreement; of goods and services
3. **出租, 租出** — grant use or occupation of under a term of contract
*“I am leasing my country estate to some foreigners”*
4. **出租, 租借, 租出, 租賃, 租** — engage for service under a term of contract
*“We took an apartment on a quiet street”*
*“Let's rent a car”*
*n.* 1. **供出租的財產, 租物** — property that is leased or rented out or let
2. **租契, 租約** — a contract granting use or occupation of property during a specified time for a specified payment

4. **租期** — the period of time during which a contract conveying property to a person is in effect

## least

**發音：** /liːst/
*adv.* 1. used to form the superlative
*“The garter snake is the least dangerous snake”*
*adj.* 1. the superlative of `little' that can be used with mass nouns and is usually preceded by `the'; a quantifier meaning smallest in amount or extent or degree
*“didn't care the least bit”*
*“he has the least talent of anyone”*
*n.* 1. something that is of no importance
*“it is the least I can do”*
*“that is the least of my concerns”*
*det.* 1. **至少, 最少, 最小** — Smallest in amount or extent.

<a id="entry-l-001059"></a>
## leave

**發音：** /ˈliːv/
*v.* 1. **動身, 走開, 出發, 走, 離開** — go away from a place
*“At what time does your train leave?”*
*“She didn't leave until midnight”*
2. **出門, 退場, 出去, 動身, 離去** — move out of or depart from
*“leave the room”*
*“the fugitive has left the country”*
3. **導致, 致使, 留** — have as a result or residue
*“The water left a mark on the silk dress”*
*“Her blood left a stain on the napkin”*
4. **離開, 脫離, 放棄** — remove oneself from an association with or participation in
*“She wants to leave”*
*“The teenager left home”*
5. **交託, 信託, 交付** — put into the care or protection of someone
*“He left the decision to his deputy”*
*“leave your child the nurse's care”*
6. **留下, 遺贈, 遺留** — leave or give by will after one's death
*“My aunt bequeathed me all her jewelry”*
*“My grandfather left me his entire estate”*
*n.* 1. **休假, 假期** — the period of time during which you are absent from work or duty
*“a ten day's leave to visit his mother”*
2. **分別, 離別, 送別, 告別** — the act of departing politely
*“he disliked long farewells”*
*“he took his leave”*
3. permission to do something
*“she was granted leave to speak”*

<a id="entry-l-001060"></a>
## leave behind

*v.* 1. **丟下, 留下** — depart and not take along
*“He left behind all his possessions when he moved to Europe”*
2. **剩下, 留, 留下** — be survived by after one's death
*“He left six children”*
*“At her death, she left behind her husband and 11 cats”*
3. *(idiomatic, transitive)* **留下** — To not live longer than; to be survived by.
4. *(idiomatic, transitive)* **留下** — To leave (a trace of something).
5. *(idiomatic, transitive)* **遺棄 /遺棄** — To abandon.
6. *(idiomatic, transitive)* **忘記 /忘記, 掉, 落** — To forget about.

<a id="entry-l-001061"></a>

## left

*“I rarely lose a left-hand glove”*

*“leftover meatloaf”*
*“she had a little money left over so she went to a movie”*
*n.* 1. **左邊** — location near or direction toward the left side; i.e. the side to the north when a person or object faces east
*“she stood on the left”*
2. **左外野** — the piece of ground in the outfield on the catcher's left
*“the batter flied out to left”*
3. **左面** — a turn toward the side of the body that is on the north when the person is facing east
*“take a left at the corner”*
4. those who support varying degrees of social or political or economic change designed to promote the public welfare
5. the hand that is on the left side of the body
*“jab with your left”*
*adv.* 1. **向左, 左派地, 左邊地, 往左** — toward or on the left; also used figuratively
*“he looked right and left”*
*“the political party has moved left”*

3. *(uncountable, usually)* **立法, 法規 /法規** — A law which has been enacted by legislature or other governing body.
4. law enacted by a legislative body

*“legislative proposal”*
3. **立法的** — Making, or having the power to make, a law or laws; lawmaking.

<a id="entry-l-001210"></a>
## legitimate

**發音：** /lɪˈd͡ʒɪt.ɪ.mət/
*adj.* 1. **合理+的, 符合邏輯+的, 有邏輯+的, 合乎邏輯+的** — based on known statements or events or conditions
*“rain was a logical expectation, given the time of year”*
2. **合法** — In accordance with the law or established legal forms and requirements.
3. of marriages and offspring; recognized as lawful
4. in accordance with recognized or accepted standards or principles
*“legitimate advertising practices”*
5. authorized, sanctioned by, or in accordance with law
*“a legitimate government”*
*v.* 1. **使合法, 使合法化, 公認, 合法化** — make legal
*“Marijuana should be legalized”*
2. show or affirm to be just and legitimate
3. make (an illegitimate child) legitimate; declare the legitimacy of (someone)
*“They legitimized their natural child”*

<a id="entry-l-001211"></a>

## lend

**發音：** /ˈlɛnd/
*v.* 1. **借, 借出, 借給, 貸款, 出租** — give temporarily; let have for a limited time
*“I will lend you my car”*
*“loan me some money”*
2. **添加, 增添, 產生, 提供, 給予** — bestow a quality on
*“Her presence lends a certain cachet to the company”*
*“The music added a lot to the play”*
3. **提供, 給予** — have certain characteristics of qualities for something; be open or vulnerable to
*“This story would lend itself well to serialization on television”*
*“The current system lends itself to great abuse”*
4. *(transitive)* **借出, 借, 貸款 /貸款** — To allow to be used by someone temporarily, on condition that it or its equivalent will be returned.

## length

**發音：** /lɛŋ(k)θ/
*n.* 1. **持續的時間, 時間的長短, 期間, 長短, 時間** — continuance in time
*“the ceremony was of short duration”*
*“he complained about the length of time required”*
2. **長度** — the property of being the extent of something from beginning to end
*“the editor limited the length of my article to 500 words”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **長度 /長度, 長 /長** — The distance measured along the longest dimension of an object.
4. **一截, 一節, 一段** — a section of something that is long and narrow
*“a length of timber”*
*“a length of tubing”*
5. the linear extent in space from one end to the other; the longest dimension of something that is fixed in place
*“the length of the table was 5 feet”*
6. size of the gap between two places
*“the distance from New York to Chicago”*
*“he determined the length of the shortest line segment joining the two points”*

<a id="entry-l-001291"></a>
## lengthen

**發音：** /ˈlɛŋθən/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **加長 /加長** — To make longer, to extend the length of.
2. make longer
*“Lengthen this skirt, please”*
3. become long or longer
*“In Spring, the days lengthen”*

<a id="entry-l-001292"></a>
## lengthened

*adj.* 1. **伸長+的, 延長+的, 擴大+的, 拉伸+的** — drawn out or made longer spatially
*“Picasso's elongated Don Quixote”*
*“lengthened skirts are fashionable this year”*

<a id="entry-l-001293"></a>
## lengthiness

*n.* 1. **冗長, 延伸, 延長** — the consequence of being lengthened in duration
2. amount or degree or range to which something extends
*“the wire has an extension of 50 feet”*

<a id="entry-l-001294"></a>
## lengthways

*adj.* 1. running or extending in the direction of the length of a thing
*“the lengthwise dimension”*
*adv.* 1. **縱向+地, 縱長+地** — in the direction of the length
*“He cut the paper lengthwise”*

<a id="entry-l-001295"></a>
## lengthwise

*adv.* 1. **縱向+地, 縱長+地** — in the direction of the length
*“He cut the paper lengthwise”*
*adj.* 1. running or extending in the direction of the length of a thing
*“the lengthwise dimension”*

<a id="entry-l-001296"></a>
## leniency

**發音：** /ˈliːni.ən(t)si/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **寬大 /寬大, 仁慈** — The quality of mercy or forgiveness, especially in the assignment of punishment as in a court case.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **寬容行為 /寬容行為** — An act of being lenient.
3. mercifulness as a consequence of being lenient or tolerant
4. a disposition to yield to the wishes of someone
*“too much indulgence spoils a child”*
5. lightening a penalty or excusing from a chore by judges or parents or teachers

<a id="entry-l-001297"></a>
## lenient

**發音：** /ˈliːni.ənt/
*adj.* 1. **寬大, 寬鬆** — Lax; not strict; tolerant of dissent or deviation.
2. **厚道+的, 寬大+的, 慈悲為懷+的, 寬容+的, 仁慈+的** — characterized by tolerance and mercy
3. tolerant or lenient
*“indulgent parents risk spoiling their children”*
*“too soft on the children”*
4. not strict
*“an easy teacher”*
*“easy standards”*

<a id="entry-l-001298"></a>
## leniently

*adv.* 1. **寬大地** — in a permissively lenient manner
*“he felt incensed that Tarrant should have been treated so leniently given his crime”*

*n.* 1. **透鏡, 鏡頭, 鏡片** — a transparent optical device used to converge or diverge transmitted light and to form images
2. **透鏡 /透鏡, 鏡片 /鏡片, 鏡頭 /鏡頭** — An object, usually made of glass, that focuses or defocuses the light that passes through it.

4. **晶狀體 /晶狀體, 水晶體 /水晶體** — The transparent crystalline structure in the eye.
5. **電子透鏡** — electronic equipment that uses a magnetic or electric field in order to focus a beam of electrons

*“the writer is the lens through which history can be seen”*

<a id="entry-l-001435"></a>
## less

**發音：** /lɛs/
*adv.* 1. *(comparative, diminutive)* **更少, 不太, 不那麼** — To a smaller extent or degree.
2. used to form the comparative of some adjectives and adverbs
*“less interesting”*
*“less expensive”*
3. comparative of little
*“she walks less than she should”*
*“he works less these days”*
*adj.* 1. **較劣+的, 低等+的, 低質量+的, 較差+的** — (usually preceded by `no') lower in quality
*“no less than perfect”*
2. (comparative of `little' usually used with mass nouns) a quantifier meaning not as great in amount or degree
*“of less importance”*
*“less time to spend with the family”*
3. (nonstandard in some uses but often idiomatic with measure phrases) fewer
*“less than three weeks”*
*“no less than 50 people attended”*

<a id="entry-l-001436"></a>
## less is more

**發音：** /lɛs ɪz ˈmɔː/
*phr.* 1. **少即是多, 物以稀為貴** — That which is less complicated is often better understood and more appreciated than what is more complicated; brevity in communication is more effective than verbosity.

<a id="entry-l-001437"></a>
## lessen

**發音：** /ˈlɛsən/
*v.* 1. **減弱, 減少, 減輕, 下降** — decrease in size, extent, or range
*“The amount of homework decreased towards the end of the semester”*
*“The cabin pressure fell dramatically”*
2. **減少, 削減** — make smaller
*“He decreased his staff”*
3. *(transitive)* **減少 /減少** — To make less; to diminish; to reduce.
4. wear off or die down
*“The pain subsided”*

<a id="entry-l-001438"></a>
## lessening

*n.* 1. **變小, 降低, 減小, 減輕, 減少** — a change downward
*“there was a decrease in his temperature as the fever subsided”*
*“there was a sharp drop-off in sales”*

## lesson

**發音：** /ˈlɛs.ən/
*n.* 1. **一堂課, 一節課** — a unit of instruction
*“he took driving lessons”*
2. **課業 /課業, -課 /-課** — A section of learning or teaching into which a wider learning content is divided.
3. punishment intended as a warning to others
*“they decided to make an example of him”*
4. the significance of a story or event
*“the moral of the story is to love thy neighbor”*
5. a task assigned for individual study
*“he did the lesson for today”*

<a id="entry-l-001458"></a>

<a id="entry-l-001459"></a>
## lest

**發音：** /lɛst/
*conj.* 1. *(formal, literary)* **以免** — For fear that; that not; in order to prevent something from happening; in case.

<a id="entry-l-001460"></a>
## let

**發音：** /lɛt/
*v.* 1. **容許, 允許, 讓, 許可** — make it possible through a specific action or lack of action for something to happen
*“This permits the water to rush in”*
*“This sealed door won't allow the water come into the basement”*
2. **允許, 容許, 讓, 許可** — consent to, give permission
*“She permitted her son to visit her estranged husband”*
*“I won't let the police search her basement”*
3. *(UK, transitive)* **出租** — To allow possession of (a property etc.) in exchange for rent.
4. **出租, 租出** — grant use or occupation of under a term of contract
*“I am leasing my country estate to some foreigners”*
5. actively cause something to happen
*“I let it be known that I was not interested”*
6. cause to move; cause to be in a certain position or condition
*“He got his squad on the ball”*
*“This let me in for a big surprise”*
*n.* 1. a brutal terrorist group active in Kashmir; fights against India with the goal of restoring Islamic rule of India
*“Lashkar-e-Toiba has committed mass murders of civilian Hindus”*
2. a serve that strikes the net before falling into the receiver's court; the ball must be served again

<a id="entry-l-001461"></a>
## let alone

*adv.* 1. much less
*“she can't boil potatoes, let alone cook a meal”*
*conj.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **更不用說 /更不用說, 何況 /何況, 遑論 /遑論** — Much less; to say nothing of; used after one negative clause to introduce another, usually broader and more important clause, whose negation is implied by the negation of the first. However either of these instances mentioned can be applied with the use of let alone.

<a id="entry-l-001462"></a>
## let be

*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **放任, 聽任 /聽任** — To not disturb or meddle with; to leave (someone or something) alone (see let it be).
2. **設...為** — Used to assign a value to a symbol.

<a id="entry-l-001463"></a>
## let bygones be bygones

**發音：** /lɛt ˈbaɪɡɒnz biː ˈbaɪɡɒnz/
*v.* 1. *(idiomatic, intransitive)* **既往不咎, 讓過去過去吧 /讓過去過去吧** — To disregard or ignore a past difficulty in a relationship or an offence (when dealing with another individual).

<a id="entry-l-001464"></a>
## let down

*v.* 1. **拿下, 放下, 放低, 降低** — move something or somebody to a lower position
*“take down the vase from the shelf”*
2. **放下, 低下, 降低** — Used other than figuratively or idiomatically: see let, down: especially, to allow to descend.
3. *(idiomatic, transitive)* **辜負 /辜負, 對不起 /對不起** — To disappoint; to betray or fail somebody.
4. **使失望** — fail to meet the hopes or expectations of
*“Her boyfriend let her down when he did not propose marriage”*

<a id="entry-l-001465"></a>
## let go

*v.* 1. **放手, 無拘無束, 鬆手** — be relaxed
*“Don't be so worried all the time--just let go!”*
2. *(intransitive, transitive, with-of)* **放開, 放開 /放開, 放手** — To release from one's grasp; to go from a state of holding on to a state of no longer holding on.
3. release, as from one's grip
*“Let go of the door handle, please!”*
*“relinquish your grip on the rope--you won't fall”*

<a id="entry-l-001466"></a>
## let in

*v.* 1. **招收, 允許進入, 接納, 接受** — allow participation in or the right to be part of; permit to exercise the rights, functions, and responsibilities of
*“admit someone to the profession”*
*“She was admitted to the New Jersey Bar”*
2. **批准進入, 接納, 允許進入** — allow to enter; grant entry to
*“We cannot admit non-members into our club building”*
*“This pipe admits air”*

<a id="entry-l-001467"></a>
## let it be

<a id="entry-l-001468"></a>
## let know

*v.* 1. *(ditransitive)* **告知, 通知, 告訴 /告訴** — To inform (someone) (of something).

<a id="entry-l-001469"></a>
## let loose

*v.* 1. **做聲, 發出** — express audibly; utter sounds (not necessarily words)
*“She let out a big heavy sigh”*
*“He uttered strange sounds that nobody could understand”*
2. *(idiomatic, transitive)* **放開 /放開** — To free; to release from restraint.
3. turn loose or free from restraint
*“let loose mines”*
*“Loose terrible plagues upon humanity”*

<a id="entry-l-001470"></a>
## let nature take its course

*v.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **順其自然 /順其自然** — To permit events to proceed or a situation to develop without intervention or interference.

<a id="entry-l-001471"></a>
## let on

*v.* 1. **揭發, 揭露, 洩露, 遁道, 露出** — make known to the public information that was previously known only to a few people or that was meant to be kept a secret
*“The auction house would not disclose the price at which the van Gogh had sold”*
*“The actress won't reveal how old she is”*

<a id="entry-l-001472"></a>
## let out

*v.* 1. **做聲, 發出** — express audibly; utter sounds (not necessarily words)
*“She let out a big heavy sigh”*
*“He uttered strange sounds that nobody could understand”*
2. **揭發, 揭露, 洩露, 遁道, 露出** — make known to the public information that was previously known only to a few people or that was meant to be kept a secret
*“The auction house would not disclose the price at which the van Gogh had sold”*
*“The actress won't reveal how old she is”*
3. bring out of a specific state
4. make (clothes) larger
*“Let out that dress--I gained a lot of weight”*

<a id="entry-l-001473"></a>
## let sleeping dogs lie

<a id="entry-l-001474"></a>
## let someone down gently

*v.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **給某人留面子 /給某人留面子, 婉轉地告訴 /婉轉地告訴** — To reject or refuse someone in a way that avoids causing hurt or disappointment.

<a id="entry-l-001475"></a>
## let the cat out of the bag

*v.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **露餡** — To disclose a secret; to let a secret be known, often inadvertently.
2. divulge confidential information or secrets
*“Be careful--his secretary talks”*

<a id="entry-l-001476"></a>
## let there be light

**發音：** /lɛt ðɛɹ bi ləɪːt/
*phr.* 1. **要有光** — Used as a metaphor for the spread of wisdom.

3. *(colloquial)* **加油, 來 /來, 乾巴得** — Yay. (an expression of pleasure, joy, relief, or satisfaction)

## letdown

*n.* 1. **失望** — a feeling of dissatisfaction that results when your expectations are not realized
*“his hopes were so high he was doomed to disappointment”*

*“deadly poisons”*

2. **致命的, 致死的** — Of, pertaining to, or causing death; deadly; mortal; fatal.

*“he won letters in three sports”*

<a id="entry-l-001551"></a>
## level

**發音：** /ˈlɛvəl/
*n.* 1. **層次, 度, 水平, 程度, 度數** — a position on a scale of intensity or amount or quality
*“a moderate grade of intelligence”*
*“a high level of care is required”*
2. **分等, 等級** — a relative position or degree of value in a graded group
*“lumber of the highest grade”*
3. **級, 程度, 階段** — a specific identifiable position in a continuum or series or especially in a process
*“a remarkable degree of frankness”*
*“at what stage are the social sciences?”*
4. **氣泡水準儀, 水準儀, 水平儀** — indicator that establishes the horizontal when a bubble is centered in a tube of liquid
5. **水平面** — a flat surface at right angles to a plumb line
*“park the car on the level”*
6. *(countable, uncountable)* **水平, 層次 /層次, 級別 /級別, 水準 /水準, 級 /級** — Degree or amount.
*v.* 1. **變為平地, 夷平, 拆卸, 拆毀, 破壞** — tear down so as to make flat with the ground
*“The building was levelled”*
2. *(transitive)* **平整** — To adjust so as to make as flat or perpendicular to the ground as possible.
3. *(transitive)* **鏟平 /剷平, 夷為平地 /夷為平地** — To destroy by reducing to ground level; to raze.
4. *(intransitive)* **升級 /升級** — To progress to the next level.
5. **使平均, 使平坦, 使成為水平, 變平, 弄平** — become level or even
*“The ground levelled off”*
6. aim at
*“level criticism or charges at somebody”*
*adj.* 1. **平穩的, 直盯著的** — not showing abrupt variations
*“spoke in a level voice”*
*“she gave him a level look"- Louis Auchincloss”*
2. **平, 平坦** — The same height at all places; parallel to a flat ground.
3. **平, 平衡** — At the same height as some reference; constructed as level with.
4. having a surface without slope, tilt in which no part is higher or lower than another
*“a flat desk”*
*“acres of level farmland”*
5. being on a precise horizontal plane
*“a billiard table must be level”*
6. oriented at right angles to the plumb
*“the picture is level”*

*n.* 1. *(UK)* **平交道口, 平交道, 鐵路道口 /鐵路道口, 平面交叉, 道口** — An at-grade crossing between a railway line and an ordinary road, with rails and road at the same level.
2. **平交路口, 平交道, 平交道口, 平面交叉, 鐵路道口** — intersection of a railway and a road on the same level; barriers close road when trains pass

*v.* 1. **使平均, 使平坦, 使成為水平, 變平, 弄平** — become level or even
*“The ground levelled off”*

## lever

**發音：** /ˈliː.və/
*n.* 1. **槓桿** — a rigid bar pivoted about a fulcrum
2. **槓桿裝置** — a simple machine that gives a mechanical advantage when given a fulcrum
3. **槓桿 /槓桿** — A small such piece to trigger or control a mechanical device (like a switch or a button).
4. **金屬製栓** — a flat metal tumbler in a lever lock
*v.* 1. to move or force, especially in an effort to get something open
*“The burglar jimmied the lock": "Raccoons managed to pry the lid off the garbage pail”*

<a id="entry-l-001558"></a>

<a id="entry-l-001559"></a>

<a id="entry-l-001560"></a>
## lever tumbler

*n.* 1. **金屬製栓** — a flat metal tumbler in a lever lock

<a id="entry-l-001561"></a>
## leverage

**發音：** /ˈlɛv.(ə.)ɹɪd͡ʒ/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **槓桿 /槓桿** — The use of borrowed funds with a contractually determined return to increase the ability to invest and earn an expected higher return, but usually at high risk.
2. the mechanical advantage gained by being in a position to use a lever
3. strategic advantage; power to act effectively
*“relatively small groups can sometimes exert immense political leverage”*
4. investing with borrowed money as a way to amplify potential gains (at the risk of greater losses)
*v.* 1. *(US, slang, transitive)* **利用** — To use; to exploit; to manipulate in order to take full advantage (of something).
2. **槓桿式投機** — supplement with leverage
*“leverage the money that is already available”*
3. **槓桿式投機** — provide with leverage
*“We need to leverage this company”*

<a id="entry-l-001562"></a>
## leveret

## leviathan

**發音：** /lɪˈvaɪəθn̩/
*n.* 1. **利維坦 /利維坦, 力威亞探 /力威亞探, 裡外雅堂** — A vast sea monster of tremendous strength, either imaginary or real, described as the most dangerous and powerful creature in the ocean.
2. *(figuratively)* **利維坦 /利維坦** — Sometimes in the form Leviathan: based on the writings of Thomas Hobbes, the political state, especially a domineering and totalitarian one.
3. the largest or most massive thing of its kind
*“it was a leviathan among redwoods”*
*“they were assigned the leviathan of textbooks”*
4. monstrous sea creature symbolizing evil in the Old Testament

<a id="entry-l-001569"></a>
## levitate

*v.* 1. **使升空, 使在空中漂浮, 使懸在空中, 使輕輕浮起, 使飄浮空中** — cause to rise in the air and float, as if in defiance of gravity
*“The magician levitated the woman”*
2. **使漂浮, 懸浮, 翱翔, 飄浮空中, 使升空** — be suspended in the air, as if in defiance of gravity
*“The guru claimed that he could levitate”*

## levy

**發音：** /ˈlɛ.vi/
*v.* 1. **徵收, 課徵** — impose and collect
*“levy a fine”*
2. *(transitive)* **課稅 /課稅** — To impose (a tax or fine) to collect monies due, or to confiscate property.
3. cause to assemble or enlist in the military
*“raise an army”*
*“recruit new soldiers”*
*n.* 1. a charge imposed and collected
2. the act of drafting into military service

<a id="entry-l-001577"></a>
## lewd

**發音：** /ljuːd/
*adj.* 1. **粗俗+的, 下流+的, 好色+的, 猥褻+的, 淫穢+的** — suggestive of or tending to moral looseness
*“lewd whisperings of a dirty old man”*
*“an indecent gesture”*
2. **好色, 淫蕩 /淫蕩** — Lascivious, sexually promiscuous, rude.
3. **好色+的, 淫蕩+的** — driven by lust; preoccupied with or exhibiting lustful desires
*“libidinous orgies”*
*n.* 1. **色圖 /色圖** — A sexually suggestive image, particularly one which does not involve full nudity.

<a id="entry-l-001578"></a>
## lewdly

*adv.* 1. **下流+地, 淫穢+地, 猥褻+地, 淫蕩+地** — in a lewd and obscene manner
*“he had seen how in their dances the white men and women held one another obscenely”*

<a id="entry-l-001579"></a>
## Lewy body

**發音：** /ˈlɛvi ˌbɒdi/
*n.* 1. **路易氏體 /路易氏體, 路易體 /路易體** — An abnormal aggregate of protein that develops inside nerve cells.
