## lex talionis

*n.* 1. **同態復仇 /同態復仇** — law of equivalent retaliation.

<a id="entry-l-001581"></a>
## lexeme

**發音：** /ˈlɛkˌsiːm/
*n.* 1. **詞素 /詞素** — An individual instance of a continuous character sequence without spaces, used in lexical analysis.
2. a minimal unit (as a word or stem) in the lexicon of a language; `go' and `went' and `gone' and `going' are all members of the English lexeme `go'

<a id="entry-l-001582"></a>
## lexical

**發音：** /ˈlɛksɪkəl/
*adj.* 1. **詞彙+的** — of or relating to words
*“lexical decision task”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **詞彙的 /詞彙的** — Concerning the vocabulary, words, sentences or morphemes of a language.
3. *(not-comparable)* **詞典的 /詞典的** — Concerning lexicography or a lexicon or dictionary.
4. **字典+的, 詞典+的** — of or relating to dictionaries

<a id="entry-l-001583"></a>
## lexical analysis

**發音：** /ˈlɛksɪkəl əˈnæləsɪs/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **詞法分析** — The conversion of a stream of characters to a stream of meaningful tokens; normally to simplify parsing.

<a id="entry-l-001584"></a>
## lexical analyzer

*n.* 1. **詞法分析器 /詞法分析器** — A computer program that performs lexical analysis.

<a id="entry-l-001585"></a>
## lexical category

*n.* 1. **詞彙範疇 /詞彙範疇, 詞類 /詞類, 詞性 /詞性** — A linguistic category of words (or more precisely lexical items), generally defined by the syntactic or morphological behaviour of the lexical item in question, such as nouns or verbs.

<a id="entry-l-001586"></a>
## lexical item

*n.* 1. **詞項 /詞項** — A term (that is, a word or sequence of words) that acts as a unit of meaning, including words, phrases, phrasal verbs, and proverbs.

<a id="entry-l-001587"></a>
## lexical scoping

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **詞法作用域 /詞法作用域** — The property of being lexically scoped.

<a id="entry-l-001588"></a>
## lexicalisation

*n.* 1. **編入詞彙** — the process of making a word to express a concept

<a id="entry-l-001589"></a>
## lexicalised

*adj.* 1. **詞彙化+的** — expressed by a word

<a id="entry-l-001590"></a>
## lexicalization

**發音：** /ˌlɛksɪkəlaɪ̯ˈzeɪ̯ʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **詞匯化 /詞彙化** — The act or process of lexicalizing.
2. **編入詞彙** — the process of making a word to express a concept

<a id="entry-l-001591"></a>
## lexicalize

**發音：** /ˈlɛksɪkəlaɪz/
*v.* 1. *(intransitive)* **詞彙化** — To become part of the vocabulary of a language as a single lexical unit with a distinct meaning.
2. make or coin into a word or accept a new word into the lexicon of a language
*“The concept expressed by German `Gemuetlichkeit' is not lexicalized in English”*

<a id="entry-l-001592"></a>
## lexicalized

*adj.* 1. **詞彙化+的** — expressed by a word

<a id="entry-l-001593"></a>
## lexicographer

**發音：** /ˌlɛksɪˈkɒɡɹəfə(ɹ)/
*n.* 1. **詞典編纂者 /詞典編纂者** — One who writes or compiles a dictionary.
2. a compiler or writer of a dictionary; a student of the lexical component of language

<a id="entry-l-001594"></a>
## lexicographic

*adj.* 1. **辭典編纂+的** — of or relating to lexicography

<a id="entry-l-001595"></a>
## lexicographical

*adj.* 1. **辭典編纂+的** — of or relating to lexicography

<a id="entry-l-001596"></a>
## lexicography

**發音：** /ˌlɛksɪˈkɒɡɹəfi/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **詞典學 /詞典學, 辭書學 /辭書學, 詞典編輯 /詞典編輯** — The art or craft of compiling, writing, and editing dictionaries.
2. the act of writing dictionaries

<a id="entry-l-001597"></a>
## lexicology

**發音：** /ˌlɛksɪˈkɒləd͡ʒi/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **詞彙學 /詞彙學** — The part of linguistics that studies words, their nature and meaning, words' elements, relations between words including semantic relations, words groups and the whole lexicon.
2. the branch of linguistics that studies the lexical component of language

<a id="entry-l-001598"></a>
## lexicon

**發音：** /ˈlɛk.sɪ.kən/
*n.* 1. **詞庫 /詞庫, 詞匯 /詞彙** — The vocabulary of a language.
2. **字典, 詞典** — a reference book containing an alphabetical list of words with information about them
3. a language user's knowledge of words

<a id="entry-l-001599"></a>
## lexicostatistic

*adj.* 1. **詞彙統計學+的** — pertaining to statistical methods used in studying the relations between languages
*“lexicostatistic techniques”*

<a id="entry-l-001626"></a>
## liable

*adj.* 1. **應受+的** — subject to legal action
*“liable to criminal charges”*
2. **不抵抗+的, 抵抗+的, 無免疫性+的, 無抵抗力+的** — (often followed by `to') likely to be affected with
*“liable to diabetes”*
3. **應負責任+的** — held legally responsible
*“men between the ages of 18 and 35 were liable for military service”*
4. at risk of or subject to experiencing something usually unpleasant
*“he is apt to lose”*
*“she is liable to forget”*

2. print slanderous statements against
*“The newspaper was accused of libeling him”*

<a id="entry-l-001657"></a>
## liberal

*n.* 1. a person who favors a political philosophy of progress and reform and the protection of civil liberties
2. a person who favors an economic theory of laissez-faire and self-regulating markets
*adj.* 1. **主張變革+的, 主張改革+的** — having political or social views favoring reform and progress
2. **不拘泥+的, 開明+的** — tolerant of change; not bound by authoritarianism, orthodoxy, or tradition
3. showing or characterized by broad-mindedness
*“a broad political stance”*
*“generous and broad sympathies”*
4. given or giving freely
*“was a big tipper”*
*“the bounteous goodness of God”*
5. not literal
*“a loose interpretation of what she had been told”*
*“a free translation of the poem”*

<a id="entry-l-001658"></a>
## liberal arts

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-only)* **通識教育 /通識教育, 博雅教育** — The academic course of instruction intended to provide general knowledge and usually comprising the arts, humanities, natural sciences, and social sciences, as opposed to professional or technical subjects.
2. *(plural, plural-only)* **三藝四術** — The trivium and the quadrivium.
3. studies intended to provide general knowledge and intellectual skills (rather than occupational or professional skills)
*“the college of arts and sciences”*

<a id="entry-l-001659"></a>

<a id="entry-l-001665"></a>
## liberalism

**發音：** /ˈlɪ.b(ə.)ɹə.lɪz.əm/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **自由主義 /自由主義** — The quality of being liberal.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **自由主義 /自由主義** — Any political movement founded on the autonomy and personal freedom of the individual, progress and reform, and government by law with the consent of the governed.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **自由主義 /自由主義** — An economic ideology in favour of laissez faire and the free market (related to economic liberalism).
4. a political orientation that favors social progress by reform and by changing laws rather than by revolution
5. an economic theory advocating free competition and a self-regulating market

<a id="entry-l-001666"></a>

## liberate

**發音：** /ˈlɪbəɹeɪt/
*v.* 1. **解放, 釋放** — give equal rights to; of women and minorities

*“The students liberated their slaves upon graduating from the university”*
4. *(ironic, sometimes, transitive)* **解放** — To free (a place such as a country, or the residents thereof) from an occupying force, or from oppression such as unjust rule.
5. *(transitive)* **釋放 /釋放** — To cause (energy or a substance) to be released through a chemical reaction or physical decomposition.
6. release (gas or energy) as a result of a chemical reaction or physical decomposition

2. **激光雷達** — a measuring system that detects and locates objects on the same principle as radar but uses light from a laser; a potential technology for detecting air turbulence that can affect aircraft

## lie

**發音：** /laɪ̯/
*v.* 1. **位於** — be located or situated somewhere; occupy a certain position
2. **保持, 處於, 繼續** — be and remain in a particular state or condition
*“lie dormant”*
3. **撒謊, 說謊, 欺騙** — tell an untruth; pretend with intent to deceive
*“Don't lie to your parents”*
*“She lied when she told me she was only 29”*
4. **在, 存在於, 位於** — have a place in relation to something else
*“The fate of Bosnia lies in the hands of the West”*
*“The responsibility rests with the Allies”*
5. **躺, 躺臥** — assume a reclining position
*“lie down on the bed until you feel better”*
6. *(intransitive)* **位於 /位於, 地處 /地處, 坐落** — To be placed or situated.
*n.* 1. **假話, 謊言, 謊話** — a statement that deviates from or perverts the truth
2. Norwegian diplomat who was the first Secretary General of the United Nations (1896-1968)
3. position or manner in which something is situated

## life

*n.* 1. **生命, 生活** — a characteristic state or mode of living
*“social life”*
*“city life”*
2. **生活** — the course of existence of an individual; the actions and events that occur in living
*“he hoped for a new life in Australia”*
*“he wanted to live his own life without interference from others”*
3. **生命, 生活** — the condition of living or the state of being alive
*“while there's life there's hope”*
*“life depends on many chemical and physical processes”*
4. **生命, 生物** — living things collectively
*“the oceans are teeming with life”*
5. **傳記, 檔案** — an account of the series of events making up a person's life
6. **無期徒刑, 終身監禁** — a prison term lasting as long as the prisoner lives
*“he got life for killing the guard”*

*“in England they call life insurance life assurance”*

2. **救生圈, 救生帶** — a life preserver in the form of a ring of buoyant material
3. belt attaching you to some object as a restraint in order to prevent you from getting hurt

## life cycle

**發音：** /ˈlaɪf ˌsaɪ.kəl/
*n.* 1. **生命週期 /生命週期** — The course of stages through which an organism passes from fertilized zygote until maturity, reproduction, and death.
2. *(broadly)* **生命週期 /生命週期** — The useful life of a product or system; the developmental history of an individual or group in society.
3. **生命週期, 生活週期** — the course of developmental changes in an organism from fertilized zygote to maturity when another zygote can be produced
4. a series of stages through which an organism passes between recurrences of a primary stage

## life support

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **生命支持系統 /生命支持系統, 生命維持系統 /生命維持系統, 生命支持設備 /生命支持設備, 人工生命維持系統 /人工生命維持系統** — An artificial system designed to provide oxygen and other conditions necessary for human life in an inhospitable environment.
2. **生命保障系統, 生命維持系統, 維生設備** — equipment that makes life possible in otherwise deadly environmental conditions
*“the astronauts relied on their life-support systems”*
3. *(uncountable)* **維生 /維生** — The maintenance of vital functions of a critically ill or comatose person or a person undergoing surgery.
4. **醫用維生設備** — medical equipment that assists or replaces important bodily functions and so enables a patient to live who otherwise might not survive
*“the patient is on life support”*
5. *(uncountable)* **維生設備 /維生設備** — The equipment and special procedures used for life support.
6. *(figuratively, uncountable)* **人工維持 /人工維持** — A means of sustaining existence or continuation of something, usually in an artificial manner when it should have changed or terminated in its natural environment.

*“Raise your hands”*
*“Lift a load”*
2. **抬, 抬起** — move upwards

<a id="entry-l-001829"></a>
## light

**發音：** /laɪt/
*n.* 1. **光源** — any device serving as a source of illumination
*“he stopped the car and turned off the lights”*
2. **亮度, 光度, 透光率** — the quality of being luminous; emitting or reflecting light
*“its luminosity is measured relative to that of our sun”*
3. **啟蒙, 啟示, 啟發** — a condition of spiritual awareness; divine illumination
*“follow God's light”*
4. **光亮, 光明** — having abundant light or illumination
*“they played as long as it was light”*
*“as long as the lighting was good”*
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **觀點 /觀點** — A point of view, or aspect from which a concept, person or thing is regarded.
6. **打火機, 點火器** — a device for lighting or igniting fuel or charges or fires
*“do you have a light?”*
*adj.* 1. **不重+的, 輕+的** — of comparatively little physical weight or density
*“a light load”*
*“magnesium is a light metal--having a specific gravity of 1.74 at 20 degrees C”*
2. **顏色淺+的** — (used of color) having a relatively small amount of coloring agent
*“light blue”*
*“light colors such as pastels”*
3. **光明, 明亮+的** — characterized by or emitting light
*“a room that is light when the shutters are open”*
*“the inside of the house was airy and light”*
4. **無重音+的** — (used of vowels or syllables) pronounced with little or no stress
*“a syllable that ends in a short vowel is a light syllable”*
*“a weak stress on the second syllable”*
5. **昏厥+的, 眩暈+的, 無力+的, 虛弱+的** — weak and likely to lose consciousness
*“suddenly felt faint from the pain”*
*“was sick and faint from hunger”*
6. **輕而薄+的** — very thin and insubstantial
*“thin paper”*
*“light summer dresses”*
*v.* 1. **點亮, 照明, 照亮** — make lighter or brighter
*“This lamp lightens the room a bit”*
2. **飛落, 下來, 降落** — to come to rest, settle
*“Misfortune lighted upon him”*
3. **使灼熱, 點起, 使燃燒, 點火, 點燃** — cause to start burning; subject to fire or great heat
*“Great heat can ignite almost any dry matter”*
*“Light a cigarette”*
4. **突然降臨, 來臨** — fall to somebody by assignment or lot
*“The task fell to me”*
*“It fell to me to notify the parents of the victims”*
5. **下馬** — alight from (a horse)
6. begin to smoke
*“After the meal, some of the diners lit up”*
*adv.* 1. **不費力+地, 容易+地, 輕便+地, 輕鬆+地** — with few burdens
*“experienced travellers travel light”*

## likable

**發音：** /ˈlaɪkəbl̩/
*adj.* 1. **動人+的, 可愛+的, 吸引人+的** — (of characters in literature or drama) evoking empathic or sympathetic feelings
*“the sympathetic characters in the play”*
2. *(US, often)* **招人喜歡 /招人喜歡** — Of a person, having qualities tending to result in being liked; friendly, personable.
3. *(US, often)* **招人喜歡 /招人喜歡** — Capable of, or suitable for, being liked.
4. easy to like; agreeable
*“an attractive and likable young man”*

<a id="entry-l-001916"></a>
## like

**發音：** /laɪ̯k/
*v.* 1. **喜歡** — be fond of
*“I like my nephews”*
2. **喜歡 /喜歡** — To find attractive; to prefer the company of; to have mild romantic feelings for.
3. *(Internet, transitive)* **點讚 /點贊, 讚 /贊** — To show support for, or approval of, something posted on the Internet by marking it with a vote.
4. prefer or wish to do something
*“Do you care to try this dish?”*
*“Would you like to come along to the movies?”*
5. find enjoyable or agreeable
*“I like jogging”*
*“She likes to read Russian novels”*
6. feel about or towards; consider, evaluate, or regard
*“How did you like the President's speech last night?”*
*adj.* 1. **相等+的, 相同+的, 同樣+的** — equal in amount or value
*“like amounts”*
*“equivalent amounts”*
2. **同樣+的, 相像+的, 相似+的, 類似+的** — having the same or similar characteristics
*“all politicians are alike”*
*“they looked utterly alike”*
3. **相像的** — Similar.
4. **相似+的, 類似+的, 一致+的** — conforming in every respect
*“boxes with corresponding dimensions”*
*“the like period of the preceding year”*
5. resembling or similar; having the same or some of the same characteristics; often used in combination
*“suits of like design”*
*“a limited circle of like minds”*
*n.* 1. *(Internet)* **贊 /贊** — An individual vote showing support for, approval of, or enjoyment of, something posted on the Internet.
2. a similar kind
*“dogs, foxes, and the like", "we don't want the likes of you around here”*
3. a kind of person
*“We'll not see his like again”*
*“I can't tolerate people of his ilk”*
*prep.* 1. **象, 相像的, 如, 猶如 /猶如** — Similar to, reminiscent of.
2. **象, 如, 好似** — Such as; including.
*part.* 1. *(colloquial)* **那個 /那個, 這個 /這個** — Used to precede an approximate quotation or paraphrase or an expression of something that happened.

## likely

*adv.* 1. with considerable certainty; without much doubt
*“He is probably out of the country”*
*“in all likelihood we are headed for war”*

*“a likely result”*

*“these services are likely to be available to us all before long”*

3. expected to become or be; in prospect

4. within the realm of credibility

## limb

**發音：** /lɪm/
*n.* 1. **肢, 翼** — one of the jointed appendages of an animal used for locomotion or grasping: arm; leg; wing; flipper
2. **肢, 手腳 /手腳, 四肢** — A major appendage of human or animal, used for locomotion (such as an arm, leg or wing).
3. **肢, 翼** — either of the two halves of a bow from handle to tip
*“the upper limb of the bow”*
4. **樹叉** — any of the main branches arising from the trunk or a bough of a tree
5. **分度弧或圓圈** — the graduated arc that is attached to an instrument for measuring angles
*“the limb of the sextant”*
6. **港灣, 臂狀物, 分支** — any projection that is thought to resemble a human arm
*“the arm of the record player”*
*“an arm of the sea”*

## limbic system

*n.* 1. **邊緣系統 /邊緣系統, 邊緣系 /邊緣系** — A part of the brain, in humans and various other animals, that is involved in emotion, motivation, and emotional association with memory; in humans it includes the amygdala, cingulate gyrus, fornicate gyrus, hippocampus, hypothalamus, mammillary body, nucleus accumbens, orbitofrontal cortex and parahippocampal gyrus.
2. a system of functionally related neural structures in the brain that are involved in emotional behavior

<a id="entry-l-001976"></a>
## limbo

**發音：** /ˈlɪmbəʊ/
*n.* 1. **湮沒, 遺忘** — the state of being disregarded or forgotten
2. *(uncountable)* **靈薄獄 /靈薄獄** — A speculative place or state, thought possibly to be on the edge of the bottomless pit of Hell, where the souls of innocent deceased people might exist temporarily until they can enter heaven, specifically those of the saints who died before the advent of Jesus Christ (who occupy the limbo patrum or limbo of the patriarchs or fathers) and those of unbaptized infants (who occupy the limbo infantum or limbo of the infants); (countable) the possible place where each category of souls might exist, regarded separately.
3. *(also, attributive)* **林波舞** — A competitive dance originating from Trinidad and Tobago in which dancers take turns to cross under a horizontal bar while bending backwards. The bar is lowered with each round, and the competition is won by the dancer who passes under the bar in the lowest position without dislodging it or falling down.
4. an imaginary place for lost or neglected things
5. (theology) in Roman Catholicism, the place of unbaptized but innocent or righteous souls (such as infants and virtuous individuals)

## limit

**發音：** /ˈlɪm.ɪt/
*v.* 1. **限制, 限定, 拘束, 束縛, 約束** — place limits on (extent or access)
*“restrict the use of this parking lot”*
*“limit the time you can spend with your friends”*
2. *(transitive)* **制限, 限制** — To restrict; to circumscribe; not to allow to go beyond a certain bound, to set boundaries.
3. restrict or confine, "I limit you to two visits to the pub a day"
4. decide upon or fix definitely
*“fix the variables”*
*“specify the parameters”*
*n.* 1. **端點, 界限, 終點** — final or latest limiting point
2. **界限** — the boundary of a specific area
3. **侷限, 限度, 極限, 限制因素, 限制** — the greatest amount of something that is possible or allowed
*“there are limits on the amount you can bet”*
*“it is growing rapidly with no limitation in sight”*
4. **極限 /極限** — A restriction; a bound beyond which one may not go or proceed.
5. **極限 /極限** — A value to which a sequence converges. Equivalently, the common value of the upper limit and the lower limit of a sequence: if the upper and lower limits are different, then the sequence has no limit (i.e., does not converge).
6. **極限** — the mathematical value toward which a function goes as the independent variable approaches infinity

<a id="entry-l-001998"></a>
## limit cycle

*n.* 1. **極限環 /極限環** — A closed trajectory in phase space of a dynamical system having the property that at least one other trajectory spirals into it either as time approaches infinity or as time approaches minus-infinity.

<a id="entry-l-001999"></a>
## limit point

*n.* 1. **極限** — the mathematical value toward which a function goes as the independent variable approaches infinity

<a id="entry-l-002000"></a>
## limitation

**發音：** /lɪmɪˈteɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **限制規定, 限制** — a principle that limits the extent of something
*“I am willing to accept certain restrictions on my movements”*
2. **侷限性, 限制, 限度** — the quality of being limited or restricted
*“it is a good plan but it has serious limitations”*
3. **侷限, 限度, 極限, 限制因素, 限制** — the greatest amount of something that is possible or allowed
*“there are limits on the amount you can bet”*
*“it is growing rapidly with no limitation in sight”*
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **限制** — The act of limiting or the state of being limited.
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **限制** — A restriction; a boundary, real or metaphorical, caused by some thing or some circumstance.
6. *(countable, uncountable)* **侷限性** — An imperfection or shortcoming that limits something's use or value.

3. **有限的** — With certain (often specified) limits placed upon it.
4. including only a part
5. mediocre
6. not excessive

## limited liability company

*n.* 1. **有限責任公司 /有限責任公司, 有限公司** — A type or form of for-profit incorporated company where ownership is divided into shares, and where the governing rules are set forth in a contract entered into by all of the initial shareholders. The name derives from the fact that regardless of potential losses or even bankruptcy of the corporation, individual shareholders will bear a maximum liability of the price they paid for their shares.

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **鉬銅礦 /鉬銅礦** — A monoclinic-prismatic mineral containing copper, hydrogen, molybdenum, and oxygen.

<a id="entry-l-002056"></a>
## line

**發音：** /laɪn/
*n.* 1. **戰鬥編隊系列, 排隊** — a formation of people or things one behind another
*“the line stretched clear around the corner”*
*“you must wait in a long line at the checkout counter”*
2. **線** — a length (straight or curved) without breadth or thickness; the trace of a moving point
3. **臺詞** — text consisting of a row of words written across a page or computer screen
*“the letter consisted of three short lines”*
*“there are six lines in every stanza”*
4. **推理** — a course of reasoning aimed at demonstrating a truth or falsehood; the methodical process of logical reasoning
*“I can't follow your line of reasoning”*
5. **電報** — a conductor for transmitting electrical or optical signals or electric power
6. **皺, 皺紋, 皺褶** — a slight depression in the smoothness of a surface
*“his face has many lines”*
*“ironing gets rid of most wrinkles”*
*v.* 1. **延伸, 排, 排成排** — be in line with; form a line along
*“trees line the riverbank”*
2. cover the interior of
*“line the gloves”*
*“line a chimney”*
3. make a mark or lines on a surface
*“draw a line”*
*“trace the outline of a figure in the sand”*
4. mark with lines
*“sorrow had lined his face”*
5. fill plentifully
*“line one's pockets”*
6. reinforce with fabric
*“lined books are more enduring”*

<a id="entry-l-002057"></a>
## line art

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **線稿 /線稿** — Images that consist of distinct straight and curved lines placed against a (usually plain) background, without gradations in shade (darkness) or hue (color).

<a id="entry-l-002058"></a>
## line block

*n.* 1. **線雕凸版** — engraving consisting of a block that has been etched or engraved

<a id="entry-l-002059"></a>
## line break

*n.* 1. **斷行 /斷行, 換行 /換行, 移行** — A point in writing where text that would normally continue on the same line starts at the beginning of a new line.

<a id="entry-l-002060"></a>
## line drawing

*n.* 1. **描寫, 概圖, 簡圖, 略圖, 示意圖** — a drawing of the outlines of forms or objects

## line engraving

2. **線雕凸版** — engraving consisting of a block that has been etched or engraved

<a id="entry-l-002063"></a>

*n.* 1. **折線圖 /折線圖, 線形圖 /線形圖** — A graph in which a series of connected straight lines connect points, each of which represents the value of a particular variable as a function of another variable.

2. administration of the activities contributing directly to an organization's output

*“they are outstanding in their field”*

<a id="entry-l-002069"></a>

<a id="entry-l-002070"></a>
## line of credit

*n.* 1. **信用額度 /信用額度, 貸款額度 /貸款額度** — A credit facility under which a bank or other lender agrees to provide a client with loans of money up to an approved limit during a predefined period. The client may borrow the entire credit amount all at once or in portions during the specified period.
2. **信貸限額, 銀行授信額度** — the maximum credit that a customer is allowed
3. **信用額度 /信用額度, 貸款額度 /貸款額度** — The maximum borrowable amount made available by that facility.

<a id="entry-l-002071"></a>
## line of defence

*n.* 1. **防衛線, 防線** — defensive structure consisting of a barrier that can be employed for defense against attack

*“police are the major line of defense against crime”*

## line up

**發音：** /laɪnˈʌp/
*v.* 1. **排成一行, 排成線, 排成行** — form a line
*“The buildings all line up neatly”*
2. **排成長隊, 排隊等候** — form a queue, form a line, stand in line
*“Customers lined up in front of the store”*
3. **校直, 對準** — place in a line or arrange so as to be parallel or straight
*“align the car with the curb”*
*“align the sheets of paper on the table”*
4. *(intransitive)* **排隊 /排隊** — To get into a line; especially, so as to wait one's turn.
5. **整隊** — arrange in ranks
*“dress troops”*
6. get something or somebody for a specific purpose

## lineage

**發音：** /ˈlɪn.i.ɪd͡ʒ/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **血統 /血統** — Descent in a line from a common progenitor; progeny; descending line of offspring or ascending line of parentage.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **行數 /行數** — A number of lines of text in a column.
3. **家族分支, 血統** — the kinship relation between an individual and the individual's progenitors
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **按行費率 /按行費率** — A fee or rate paid per line of text.
5. **行數** — the number of lines in a piece of printed material
6. the descendants of one individual
*“his entire lineage has been warriors”*

*“she is related lineally to the Royal Family”*

## lineament

*“an expression of pleasure crossed his features”*
*“his lineaments were very regular”*
2. a characteristic property that defines the apparent individual nature of something
*“each town has a quality all its own”*
*“the radical character of our demands”*

<a id="entry-l-002096"></a>
## linear

**發音：** /ˈlɪn.i.ə/
*adj.* 1. **直線+的, 線性+的** — of or in or along or relating to a line; involving a single dimension
*“a linear measurement”*
2. **線性** — Having the form of a line; straight or roughly straight; following a direct course.
3. **長窄形+的** — (of a leaf shape) long and narrow
4. designating or involving an equation whose terms are of the first degree
5. of a circuit or device having an output that is proportional to the input
*“analogue device”*
*“linear amplifier”*
6. measured lengthwise
*“cost of lumber per running foot”*

<a id="entry-l-002097"></a>
## Linear A

*prop. n.* 1. **線形文字A /線形文字A** — A syllabary used to write the as-yet-undeciphered Minoan language, and an apparent predecessor to other scripts, such as Linear B.

<a id="entry-l-002098"></a>
## linear algebra

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **線性代數 /線性代數, 線代 /線代** — The branch of mathematics that deals with vectors, vector spaces, linear transformations and systems of linear equations.
2. the part of algebra that deals with the theory of linear equations and linear transformation

<a id="entry-l-002099"></a>
## Linear B

*prop. n.* 1. **線形文字B /線形文字B** — A syllabary used to write Mycenaean Greek in the second millennium BC, before the invention of the Greek alphabet.

<a id="entry-l-002100"></a>
## linear combination

*n.* 1. **線性組合 /線性組合** — a sum, each of whose summands is an appropriate vector times an appropriate scalar (or ring element)

<a id="entry-l-002101"></a>
## linear function

*n.* 1. **線性函數 /線性函數** — Any function whose graph is a straight line: f(x)=ax+b

<a id="entry-l-002102"></a>
## linear leaf

*n.* 1. **線狀葉** — a long slender leaf

<a id="entry-l-002103"></a>
## linear logic

*n.* 1. **線性邏輯 /線性邏輯** — A logic in which two structural rules are missing from its sequent calculus: those for weakening and contraction; which has some extra logical connectives, so that it has both "additive" and "multiplicative" versions of the typical binary connectives and truth constants; and which has a pair of modal, "exponential" operators for resource management, to help make up for the loss of the two structural rules.

<a id="entry-l-002104"></a>
## linear measure

*n.* 1. **線性單位, 長度度量** — a unit of measurement of length

<a id="entry-l-002105"></a>
## linear metre

*n.* 1. *(UK)* **延米** — A standard unit of length, symbol m, equal to one metre in length.

<a id="entry-l-002106"></a>
## linear operator

*n.* 1. **線性運算符** — an operator that obeys the distributive law: A(f+g) = Af + Ag (where f and g are functions)

<a id="entry-l-002107"></a>
## linear programming

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **線性規劃 /線性規劃** — The branch of mathematics concerned with the minimization or maximization of a linear function of several variables and inequalities; used in many branches of industry to minimize costs or maximize production.
2. a mathematical technique used in economics; finds the maximum or minimum of linear functions in many variables subject to constraints

<a id="entry-l-002108"></a>
## linear system

*n.* 1. **線性系統 /線性系統** — A mathematical model of a system based on the use of a linear operator.

<a id="entry-l-002109"></a>
## linear unit

*n.* 1. **線性單位, 長度度量** — a unit of measurement of length

<a id="entry-l-002110"></a>

2. the property of having one dimension

<a id="entry-l-002136"></a>
## lingua franca

**發音：** /ˈlɪŋ.ɡwə ˈfɹæŋ.kə/
*n.* 1. **通用語 /通用語, 交際語 /交際語, 共通語 /共通語, 共同語言 /共同語言, 共同語 /共同語** — A common language used by people of diverse backgrounds to communicate with one another, often a basic form of speech with simplified grammar, particularly, one that is not the first language of any of its speakers.
2. a common language used by speakers of different languages
*“Koine is a dialect of ancient Greek that was the lingua franca of the empire of Alexander the Great and was widely spoken throughout the eastern Mediterranean area in Roman times”*

<a id="entry-l-002137"></a>

<a id="entry-l-002138"></a>
## lingual

*adj.* 1. **語言+的** — consisting of or related to language
*“linguistic behavior”*
*“a linguistic atlas”*
2. *(not-comparable, relational)* **舌的** — Related to, near, or on the side toward the tongue.
3. **舌+的, 舌旁+的, 舌音+的** — pertaining to or resembling or lying near the tongue
*“lingual inflammation”*
*“the lingual surface of the teeth”*
*n.* 1. **舌音** — A sound articulated with the tongue.
2. a consonant that is produced with the tongue and other speech organs

## lingual artery

## link

**發音：** /ˈlɪŋk/
*v.* 1. **使發生聯繫, 連接, 鏈接, 連結, 聯合** — make a logical or causal connection
*“I cannot connect these two pieces of evidence in my mind”*
*“colligate these facts”*
2. **接合, 聯結, 鏈接, 連結, 使聯合** — connect, fasten, or put together two or more pieces
*“Can you connect the two loudspeakers?”*
*“Tie the ropes together”*
3. **挽, 聯, 聯合, 聯繫, 連** — be or become joined or united or linked
*“The two streets connect to become a highway”*
*“Our paths joined”*
4. link with or as with a yoke
*“yoke the oxen together”*
*n.* 1. **聯合, 聯結, 連結** — the means of connection between things linked in series
2. **鏈環, 連接物, 聯合, 連結** — a fastener that serves to join or connect
*“the walls are held together with metal links placed in the wet mortar during construction”*
3. **連結 /連結** — One element of a chain or other connected series.
4. **挽, 連結, 聯接, 連接, 聯合** — the state of being connected
*“the connection between church and state is inescapable”*
5. **連接物, 紐帶** — a connecting shape
6. **令** — a unit of length equal to 1/100 of a chain

*n.* 1. **脂類 /脂類, 脂質 /脂質** — Any of a group of organic compounds including the fats, oils, waxes, sterols, and triglycerides. Lipids are characterized by being insoluble in water.
2. an oily organic compound insoluble in water but soluble in organic solvents; essential structural component of living cells (along with proteins and carbohydrates)

<a id="entry-l-002242"></a>
## lipid granulomatosis

*n.* 1. **黃瘤病** — widespread xanthomas (especially on elbows and knees); often associated with a disorder of lipid metabolism

<a id="entry-l-002243"></a>
## lipid-lowering medication

*n.* 1. **抑制素** — a medicine that lowers blood cholesterol levels by inhibiting HMG-CoA reductase

<a id="entry-l-002244"></a>

<a id="entry-l-002284"></a>
## liquid

**發音：** /ˈlɪk.wɪd/
*adj.* 1. **液體+的, 流動+的** — existing as or having characteristics of a liquid; especially tending to flow
*“water and milk and blood are liquid substances”*
2. **充滿水+的** — filled or brimming with tears
*“swimming eyes”*
*“sorrow made the eyes of many grow liquid”*
3. **清澄+的, 清澈+的, 明亮+的, 透明+的** — clear and bright
*“the liquid air of a spring morning”*
*“eyes shining with a liquid luster”*
4. *(physical)* **液體 /液體, 液態 /液態** — Flowing freely like water; fluid; not solid and not gaseous; composed of particles that move freely among each other on the slightest pressure.
5. changed from a solid to a liquid state
*“rivers filled to overflowing by melted snow”*
6. smooth and flowing in quality; entirely free of harshness
*“the liquid song of a robin”*
*n.* 1. **液態** — the state in which a substance exhibits a characteristic readiness to flow with little or no tendency to disperse and relatively high incompressibility
2. **液體** — fluid matter having no fixed shape but a fixed volume
3. *(countable, physical, uncountable)* **液體 /液體, 液態 /液態** — A substance that is flowing, and keeping no shape, such as water; a substance of which the molecules, while not tending to separate from one another like those of a gas, readily change their relative position, and which therefore retains no definite shape, except that determined by the containing receptacle; an inelastic fluid.
4. a substance that is liquid at room temperature and pressure
5. a frictionless continuant that is not a nasal consonant (especially `l' and `r')

## liquidate

*v.* 1. **償付, 清償** — eliminate by paying off (debts)
2. **變賣** — convert into cash
*“I had to liquidate my holdings to pay off my ex-husband”*
3. **清算, 清理** — settle the affairs of by determining the debts and applying the assets to pay them off
*“liquidate a company”*
4. get rid of (someone who may be a threat) by killing
*“The mafia liquidated the informer”*
*“the double agent was neutralized”*

<a id="entry-l-002299"></a>
## liquidated damages

3. (law) a person (usually appointed by a court of law) who liquidates assets or preserves them for the benefit of affected parties

<a id="entry-l-002302"></a>
## liquidity

**發音：** /lɪkˈwɪdəti/
*n.* 1. **液態** — the state in which a substance exhibits a characteristic readiness to flow with little or no tendency to disperse and relatively high incompressibility
2. *(countable)* **流動性 /流動性** — An asset's property of being able to be sold without affecting its value; the degree to which it can be easily converted into cash.
3. the property of flowing easily
*“adding lead makes the alloy easier to cast because the melting point is reduced and the fluidity is increased”*
*“they believe that fluidity increases as the water gets warmer”*
4. being in cash or easily convertible to cash; debt paying ability

## list

*n.* 1. **列表, 單子** — a database containing an ordered array of items (names or topics)
2. **傾向, 傾斜** — the property possessed by a line or surface that departs from the vertical
*“the tower had a pronounced tilt”*
*“the ship developed a list to starboard”*
*v.* 1. **列於表上, 記入名單內** — include in a list
*“Am I listed in your register?”*
2. **使傾斜** — cause to lean to the side
*“Erosion listed the old tree”*
3. **傾斜** — tilt to one side
*“The balloon heeled over”*
*“the wind made the vessel heel”*
4. give or make a list of; name individually; give the names of
*“List the states west of the Mississippi”*
5. enumerate
*“We must number the names of the great mathematicians”*

2. **標價, 目錄價格, 訂價** — the selling price of something as stated in a catalogue or price list; often subject to discounts
*“I got it at 30% off the list price”*

*“Heed the advice of the old men”*

## literal

**發音：** /ˈlɪt(ə)ɹəl/
*adj.* 1. **實際+的** — being or reflecting the essential or genuine character of something
*“her actual motive”*
*“a literal solitude like a desert"- G.K.Chesterton”*
2. **字面, 逐字逐句** — Exactly as stated; read or understood without interpretation; according to the letter; not figurative or metaphorical; following the letter or exact words; not taking liberties; etymonic rather than idiomatic.
3. without interpretation or embellishment
*“a literal depiction of the scene before him”*
4. limited to the explicit meaning of a word or text
*“a literal translation”*
5. avoiding embellishment or exaggeration (used for emphasis)
*“it's the literal truth”*

<a id="entry-l-002413"></a>
## little

*adj.* 1. **小+的, 小型+的** — limited or below average in number or quantity or magnitude or extent
*“a little dining room”*
*“a little house”*
2. **矮+的, 短+的** — low in stature; not tall
*“he was short and stocky”*
*“short in stature”*
3. (quantifier used with mass nouns) small in quantity or degree; not much or almost none or (with `a') at least some
*“little rain fell in May”*
*“gave it little thought”*
4. (of children and animals) young, immature
*“what a big little boy you are”*
*“small children”*
5. (informal) small and of little importance
*“a fiddling sum of money”*
*“a footling gesture”*
6. (of a voice) faint
*“a little voice”*
*“a still small voice”*
*adv.* 1. not much
*“he talked little about his family”*
*n.* 1. **少量** — a small amount or duration
*“he accepted the little they gave him”*

<a id="entry-l-002473"></a>
## livable

*adj.* 1. **宜居** — Endurable, survivable, suitable for living in, inhabitable.
2. fit or suitable to live in or with
*“livable conditions”*

<a id="entry-l-002474"></a>
## live

*v.* 1. **居住於, 住** — inhabit or live in; be an inhabitant of
*“People lived in Africa millions of years ago”*
*“The people inhabited the islands that are now deserted”*
2. **居住, 生活, 過著, 過, 度過** — lead a certain kind of life; live in a certain style
*“we had to live frugally after the war”*
3. **過著, 忍耐, 忍受, 持續** — continue to live through hardship or adversity
*“We went without water and food for 3 days”*
*“These superstitions survive in the backwaters of America”*
4. **生存** — support oneself
*“he could barely exist on such a low wage”*
*“Can you live on $2000 a month in New York City?”*
5. **活, 是** — have life, be alive
*“Our great leader is no more”*
*“My grandfather lived until the end of war”*
6. **生存, 過著** — pursue a positive and satisfying existence
*“You must accept yourself and others if you really want to live”*
*adj.* 1. **實況播送+的, 實況轉播+的, 現場直播+的** — actually being performed at the time of hearing or viewing
*“a live television program”*
*“brought to you live from Lincoln Center”*
2. **活著+的** — possessing life
*“the happiest person alive”*
*“the nerve is alive”*
3. **高迴響+的** — highly reverberant
*“a live concert hall”*
4. **精力充沛+的, 活潑+的** — abounding with life and energy
*“the club members are a really live bunch”*
5. **充電+的, 有電+的, 有電能+的, 通電+的, 帶電+的** — charged or energized with electricity
*“a hot wire”*
*“a live wire”*
6. exerting force or containing energy
*“live coals”*
*“tossed a live cigarette out the window”*
*adv.* 1. not recorded
*“the opera was broadcast live”*

<a id="entry-l-002475"></a>

## lively

*“a lively discussion”*
*“lively and attractive parents”*
2. **活躍+的, 活潑+的** — full of zest or vigor
*“a racy literary style”*

*“a brisk walk in the park”*
*“a lively gait”*
4. **栩栩如生+的, 生氣蓬勃+的, 活躍+的, 活潑+的** — filled with events or activity
*“a lively period in history”*

*“clean bouncy hair”*

*“a vital and charismatic leader”*

## liver

2. liver of an animal used as meat
3. a person who has a special life style

*“a liver in cities”*

## livid

**發音：** /ˈlɪvɪd/
*adj.* 1. **無血色+的, 沒有精神+的, 鐵青+的, 蒼白+的** — anemic looking from illness or emotion
*“a face turned ashen”*
*“the invalid's blanched cheeks”*
2. **鐵青 /鐵青** — Having a dark, bluish appearance.
3. *(informal)* **怒氣沖沖 /怒氣衝衝, 大發雷霆 /大發雷霆** — So angry that one turns pale; very angry; furious; liverish.
4. (of a light) imparting a deathlike luminosity
*“livid lightning streaked the sky”*
*“a thousand flambeaux...turned all at once that deep gloom into a livid and preternatural day"- E.A.Poe”*
5. furiously angry
*“willful stupidity makes him absolutely livid”*
6. discolored by coagulation of blood beneath the skin
*“beaten black and blue”*
*“livid bruises”*

<a id="entry-l-002519"></a>

2. a state of fury so great the face becomes discolored
3. unnatural lack of color in the skin (as from bruising or sickness or emotional distress)

<a id="entry-l-002520"></a>
## lividly

<a id="entry-l-002521"></a>
## living

**發音：** /ˈlɪvɪŋ/
*adj.* 1. **有生命+的, 未死+的, 活+的, 生活+的, 活著+的** — pertaining to living persons
*“within living memory”*
2. **忠於生活的, 栩栩如生的, 逼真+的** — true to life; lifelike
*“the living image of her mother”*
3. **完全+的, 絕對+的** — (informal) absolute
*“she is a living doll”*
*“scared the living daylights out of them”*
4. *(not-comparable)* **生活** — Having life; alive.
5. still in existence
*“the Wollemi pine found in Australia is a surviving specimen of a conifer thought to have been long extinct and therefore known as a living fossil”*
*“the only surviving frontier blockhouse in Pennsylvania”*
6. still in active use
*“a living language”*
*n.* 1. **生命, 生活** — the condition of living or the state of being alive
*“while there's life there's hope”*
*“life depends on many chemical and physical processes”*
2. **生計** — the financial means whereby one lives
*“each child was expected to pay for their keep”*
*“he applied to the state for support”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **生活方式, 生態 /生態, 方式, 生活** — A style of life.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **生計 /生計, 飯碗 /飯碗** — Financial means; a means of maintaining life; livelihood
5. the experience of being alive; the course of human events and activities
*“he could no longer cope with the complexities of life”*
6. people who are still living
*“save your pity for the living”*

## load

**發音：** /loʊd/
*n.* 1. **負荷, 負載量, 負載** — weight to be borne or conveyed
2. **負載, 載荷** — a quantity that can be processed or transported at one time
*“the system broke down under excessive loads”*
3. **船貨, 裝載物** — goods carried by a large vehicle
4. **擔子 /擔子, 負荷 /負荷, 負擔 /負擔, 包袱** — A burden; a weight to be carried.
5. **載重量 /載重量** — A certain number of articles or quantity of material that can be transported or processed at one time.
6. *(colloquial, in-plural, often)* **大量, 一大堆** — A large number or amount.
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **裝載 /裝載** — To put a load on or in (a means of conveyance or a place of storage).
2. **裝載** — put (something) on a structure or conveyance
*“load the bags onto the trucks”*
3. *(transitive)* **裝 /裝, 裝填 /裝填** — To fill (a firearm or artillery) with munition.
4. *(transitive)* **加載 /加載, 載入 /載入** — To read (data or a program) from a storage medium into computer memory.
5. fill or place a load on
*“load a car”*
*“load the truck with hay”*
6. provide (a device) with something necessary
*“He loaded his gun carefully”*
*“load the camera”*

*“an affluent banker”*

3. **遊說團, 遊說集團** — a group of people who try actively to influence legislation
4. the people who support some common cause or business or principle or sectional interest
*v.* 1. **遊說, 遊說議員** — detain in conversation by or as if by holding on to the outer garments of; as for political or economic favors

## lobotomy

**發音：** /ləˈbɒtəmi/
*n.* 1. *(historical)* **腦葉切除術 /腦葉切除術, 前腦葉白質切除術 /前腦葉白質切除術** — A surgical operation involving cutting into a lobe of a body organ, specifically (neurosurgery), a procedure, now largely disused, involving severing connections between the prefrontal cortex and the thalamic region of the frontal lobe of the brain to treat certain mental illnesses.
2. surgical interruption of nerve tracts to and from the frontal lobe of the brain; often results in marked cognitive and personality changes

**發音：** /ˈləʊ.kl̩/

*“local customs”*

3. **本地** — Connected directly to a particular computer, processor, etc.; able to be accessed offline.

*“local taxes”*
*“local authorities”*
6. affecting only a restricted part or area of the body
*“local anesthesia”*

*“the local seemed to take forever to get to New York”*

*prop. n.* 1. **本地泡, 本地腔** — A cavity in the interstellar medium of the Orion Arm of the Milky Way estimated to be around 1,000 light years in diameter, with the Solar System sitting close to its centre; likely to be the result of a supernova which exploded in the last ten to twenty million years.

*“The film is set in Africa”*

<a id="entry-l-002631"></a>

*“The infection has localized in the left eye”*

*“The film is set in Africa”*

*“Our sense of sight enables us to locate objects in space”*
*“Locate the boundaries of the property”*

## logic

**發音：** /ˈlɒd͡ʒ.ɪk/
*n.* 1. **推理原則, 論理, 邏輯** — the principles that guide reasoning within a given field or situation
*“economic logic requires it”*
*“by the logic of war”*
2. *(uncountable)* **邏輯 /邏輯, 論理 /論理** — A method of human thought that involves thinking in a linear, step-by-step manner about how a problem can be solved. Logic is the basis of many principles including the scientific method.
3. *(uncountable)* **論理學 /論理學** — The mathematical study of relationships between rigorously defined concepts and of mathematical proof of statements.
4. the branch of philosophy that analyzes inference
5. reasoned and reasonable judgment
*“it made a certain kind of logic”*
6. the system of operations performed by a computer that underlies the machine's representation of logical operations

<a id="entry-l-002721"></a>
## logic analyzer

*n.* 1. **邏輯分析儀 /邏輯分析儀** — A device used to test and diagnose digital electronic systems.

<a id="entry-l-002722"></a>
## logic element

*n.* 1. **邏輯元件** — an electronic device that performs an elementary logic operation

<a id="entry-l-002723"></a>
## logic gate

*n.* 1. **門電路** — a computer circuit with several inputs but only one output that can be activated by particular combinations of inputs

<a id="entry-l-002724"></a>
## logic operation

*n.* 1. **邏輯操作, 邏輯運算** — an operation that follows the rules of symbolic logic

<a id="entry-l-002725"></a>
## logical

*adj.* 1. **合乎邏輯+的, 有邏輯+的, 有邏輯頭腦+的, 符合邏輯+的** — capable of or reflecting the capability for correct and valid reasoning
*“a logical mind”*
2. **合理+的, 符合邏輯+的, 有邏輯+的, 合乎邏輯+的** — based on known statements or events or conditions
*“rain was a logical expectation, given the time of year”*
3. marked by an orderly, logical, and aesthetically consistent relation of parts
*“a coherent argument”*
4. capable of thinking and expressing yourself in a clear and consistent manner
*“a lucid thinker”*
*“she was more coherent than she had been just after the accident”*

<a id="entry-l-002726"></a>

*“I can't follow your line of reasoning”*

<a id="entry-l-002727"></a>

<a id="entry-l-002728"></a>
## logical implication

<a id="entry-l-002729"></a>
## logical operation

*n.* 1. **邏輯操作, 邏輯運算** — an operation that follows the rules of symbolic logic

<a id="entry-l-002730"></a>

<a id="entry-l-002731"></a>

<a id="entry-l-002732"></a>

*“the lone doctor in the entire county”*
*“a lonesome pine”*

<a id="entry-l-002800"></a>
## lonely

**發音：** /ˈləʊnli/
*adj.* 1. **獨居+的, 孤獨+的, 單獨+的** — lacking companions or companionship
*“he was alone when we met him”*
*“she is alone much of the time”*
2. **寂寞, 孤獨 /孤獨** — Unhappy due to feeling isolated from contact with other people.
3. **荒蕪 /荒蕪, 人跡罕至 /人跡罕至, 孤寂** — Unfrequented by people; desolate.
4. **孤獨+的, 隱士般+的, 獨居+的, 隱士+的** — characterized by or preferring solitude
*“a lone wolf”*
*“a lonely existence”*
5. **人跡稀少+的, 荒僻+的, 荒無人煙的, 人跡罕至+的** — devoid of creatures
*“a lonely crossroads”*
*“a solitary retreat”*
6. marked by dejection from being alone
*“felt sad and lonely”*
*“the loneliest night of the week”*

## long

**發音：** /lɒŋ/
*adj.* 1. **久, 冗長+的, 較長期間+的, 長久+的, 長時間+的** — primarily temporal sense; being or indicating a relatively great or greater than average duration or passage of time or a duration as specified
*“a long life”*
*“a long boring speech”*
2. **長+的** — primarily spatial sense; of relatively great or greater than average spatial extension or extension as specified
*“a long road”*
*“a long distance”*
3. **相對高+的** — of relatively great height
*“a race of long gaunt men"- Sherwood Anderson”*
*“looked out the long French windows”*
4. **久, 長 /長** — Having great duration.
5. **有記性+的, 能記住+的, 記憶力強+的, 記性好+的, 記性強+的** — good at remembering
*“a retentive mind”*
*“tenacious memory”*
6. **冒險的** — involving substantial risk
*“long odds”*
*adv.* 1. for an extended time or at a distant time
*“a promotion long overdue”*
*“something long hoped for”*
2. for an extended distance
*v.* 1. **渴望** — desire strongly or persistently
2. *(intransitive)* **渴望** — To await, aspire, desire greatly (something to occur or to be true).
3. *(transitive)* **做多** — To take a long position in.

<a id="entry-l-002947"></a>
## look

*v.* 1. **望, 瞧, 看, 注視** — perceive with attention; direct one's gaze towards
*“She looked over the expanse of land”*
*“Look at your child!”*
2. **像是, 看起來, 似乎, 好像, 顯得** — give a certain impression or have a certain outward aspect
*“She seems to be sleeping”*
*“This appears to be a very difficult problem”*
3. **搜尋, 搜查, 搜索** — search or seek
*“We looked all day and finally found the child in the forest”*
*“Look elsewhere for the perfect gift!”*
4. **向, 朝, 面對, 面向, 面對著** — be oriented in a certain direction, often with respect to another reference point; be opposite to
*“The house looks north”*
*“My backyard look onto the pond”*

## lose

**發音：** /luːz/
*v.* 1. **丟失, 喪失** — fail to keep or to maintain; cease to have, either physically or in an abstract sense
*“She lost her purse when she left it unattended on her seat”*
2. **未能獲勝, 失敗** — fail to win
*“We lost the battle but we won the war”*
3. **放錯地方, 誤放** — place (something) where one cannot find it again
*“I misplaced my eyeglasses”*
4. **丟失, 喪失** — miss from one's possessions; lose sight of
*“I've lost my glasses again!”*
5. **看不到, 看不見** — allow to go out of sight
*“The detective lost the man he was shadowing after he had to stop at a red light”*
6. **虧損, 損失** — fail to make money in a business; make a loss or fail to profit
*“I lost thousands of dollars on that bad investment!”*
*“The company turned a loss after the first year”*

## loss

**發音：** /lɒs/
*n.* 1. **失去的東西, 失物, 損失** — something that is lost
*“the car was a total loss”*
*“loss of livestock left the rancher bankrupt”*
2. **減少, 損失, 降低** — gradual decline in amount or activity
*“weight loss”*
*“a serious loss of business”*
3. **透支, 赤字** — the amount by which the cost of a business exceeds its revenue
*“the company operated at a loss last year”*
*“the company operated in the red last year”*
4. *(countable)* **損失 /損失** — The result of no longer possessing an object, a function, or a characteristic due to external causes or misplacement.
5. *(countable)* **損失 /損失** — The death of a person or animal.
6. *(countable)* **虧損 /虧損** — The sum an entity loses on balance.

*adj.* 1. **有信號損耗的 /有信號損耗的** — Of a communication channel, subject to loss of signal strength.
2. **有功率損耗的 /有功率損耗的** — Of an electricity transmission line, subject to various forms of power loss.

<a id="entry-l-003082"></a>
## lost

**發音：** /lɒst/
*adj.* 1. **喪失+的, 失去+的, 遺失+的** — no longer in your possession or control; unable to be found or recovered
*“a lost child”*
*“lost friends”*
2. **不知所措+的, 困惑+的, 迷惑+的** — spiritually or physically doomed or destroyed
*“lost souls”*
*“a lost generation”*
3. **不再擁有的, 丟失的, 喪失的, 遺失的, 失去+的** — incapable of being recovered or regained
*“his lost honor”*
4. **被遺失 /被遺失** — In an unknown location; unable to be found.
5. **一心一意+的, 出神+的** — deeply absorbed in thought
*“as distant and bemused as a professor listening to the prattling of his freshman class”*
*“lost in thought”*
6. having lost your bearings; confused as to time or place or personal identity
*“I frequently find myself disoriented when I come up out of the subway”*
*“the anesthetic left her completely disoriented”*
*n.* 1. people who are destined to die soon
*“the agony of the doomed was in his voice”*

## lot

*n.* 1. **書庫, 批, 堆** — (often followed by `of') a large number or amount or extent
*“a batch of letters”*
*“a deal of trouble”*
2. **場地** — a parcel of land having fixed boundaries
*“he bought a lot on the lake”*
3. **命運, 宿命, 氣數, 運命, 運氣** — your overall circumstances or condition in life (including everything that happens to you)
*“whatever my fortune may be”*
*“deserved a better fate”*
4. **籤** — anything (straws or pebbles etc.) taken or chosen at random
*“the luck of the draw”*
*“they drew lots for it”*
5. an unofficial association of people or groups
*“the smart set goes there”*
*“they were an angry lot”*
6. any collection in its entirety
*“she bought the whole caboodle”*
*v.* 1. **劃分** — divide into lots, as of land, for example
2. **施予, 給予, 分發, 分配** — administer or bestow, as in small portions
*“administer critical remarks to everyone present”*
*“dole out some money”*

## low

**發音：** /ˈləʊ/
*adj.* 1. **低+的, 低垂+的, 低矮+的, 下面+的, 矮+的** — literal meanings; being at or having a relatively small elevation or upward extension
*“low ceilings”*
*“low clouds”*
2. **下賤的, 低級+的, 卑下的, 卑劣的, 卑賤的** — unrefined in character
*“low comedy”*
3. **低劣+的, 卑鄙+的, 卑鄙無恥, 浮渣一般+的, 卑微+的** — of the most contemptible kind
*“abject cowardice”*
*“a low stunt to pull”*
4. **低, 矮** — Situated close to, or even below, the ground or another normal reference plane; not high or lofty.
5. **低下+的, 卑下+的, 謙卑+的, 謙虛+的, 謙遜+的** — low or inferior in station or quality
*“a humble cottage”*
*“a lowly parish priest”*
6. **不足+的, 廢棄+的, 耗盡+的** — no longer sufficient
*“supplies are low”*
*“our funds are depleted”*
*adv.* 1. **低垂+地, 低懸+地, 低水平+地, 處於低點+地, 接近地平線+地** — in a low position; near the ground
*“the branches hung low”*
*n.* 1. **低氣壓** — an air mass of lower pressure; often brings precipitation
*“a low moved in over night bringing sleet and snow”*
2. **一檔, 低速檔, 頭檔, 第一檔** — the lowest forward gear ratio in the gear box of a motor vehicle; used to start a car moving
3. British political cartoonist (born in New Zealand) who created the character Colonel Blimp (1891-1963)
4. a low level or position or degree
*“the stock market fell to a new low”*
*v.* 1. make a low noise, characteristic of bovines

<a id="entry-l-003389"></a>
## lull

**發音：** /lʌl/
*n.* 1. **停頓** — a pause during which things are calm or activities are diminished
*“there was never a letup in the noise”*
2. **安靜的天氣** — a period of calm weather
*“there was a lull in the storm”*
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **催眠** — To cause to rest by soothing influences; to compose; to calm
2. **使安靜, 冷靜, 使平靜, 使鎮定** — make calm or still
*“quiet the dragons of worry and fear”*
3. calm by deception
*“Don't let yourself be lulled into a false state of security”*
4. become quiet or less intensive
*“the fighting lulled for a moment”*

## lurch

**發音：** /lɜːt͡ʃ/
*v.* 1. **蹣跚而行, 顛簸著行進, 搖晃** — move slowly and unsteadily
*“The truck lurched down the road”*
2. **巡行, 徘徊** — loiter about, with no apparent aim
3. walk as if unable to control one's movements
*“The drunken man staggered into the room”*
4. move abruptly
*“The ship suddenly lurched to the left”*
5. defeat by a lurch
*n.* 1. **踉蹌** — A sudden or unsteady movement.
2. an unsteady uneven gait
3. a decisive defeat in a game (especially in cribbage)
4. abrupt up-and-down motion (as caused by a ship or other conveyance)
*“the pitching and tossing was quite exciting”*
5. the act of moving forward suddenly

<a id="entry-l-003502"></a>
## lure

**發音：** /lʊə/
*v.* 1. **誘惑, 引誘** — provoke someone to do something through (often false or exaggerated) promises or persuasion
*“He lured me into temptation”*
2. *(transitive)* **誘惑 /誘惑** — To attract by temptation, appeal, or guile.
*n.* 1. **誘餌, 餌, 誘惑** — anything that serves as an enticement
2. **引誘物, 誘入圈套的物, 魚餌, 誘惑物, 餌** — something used to lure fish or other animals into danger so they can be trapped or killed
3. qualities that attract by seeming to promise some kind of reward

<a id="entry-l-003503"></a>
## lurid

**發音：** /ˈl(j)ʊə.ɹɪd/
*adj.* 1. **聳人聽聞+的, 駭人聽聞+的, 可怕+的** — horrible in fierceness or savagery
*“lurid crimes”*
*“a lurid life”*
2. **聳人聽聞+的, 駭人聽聞+的, 令人震驚+的, 可怕+的** — glaringly vivid and graphic; marked by sensationalism
*“lurid details of the accident”*
3. **駭人聽聞, 駭人** — Pruriently detailed and sensationalistic about something shocking or horrifying, especially with regard to violence or sex.
4. **蒼白+的** — ghastly pale
*“moonlight gave the statue a lurid luminence”*
5. shining with an unnatural red glow as of fire seen through smoke
*“a lurid sunset”*
*“lurid flames”*

<a id="entry-l-003504"></a>
## luridly

*adv.* 1. **聳人聽聞地, 駭人聽聞+地, 可怕+地** — in a lurid manner
*“it was luridly described in the book as the place where mystics took refuge”*

<a id="entry-l-003505"></a>

3. **伏擊, 埋伏** — wait in hiding to attack

## luxuriant

**發音：** /lʌɡˈzjʊəɹi.ənt/
*adj.* 1. **豐富 /豐富, 鬱 /鬱, 蔚, 茂, 繁茂, 莽莽** — Abundant in growth or detail.
2. marked by complexity and richness of detail
*“an elaborate lace pattern”*
3. displaying luxury and furnishing gratification to the senses
*“an epicurean banquet”*
*“enjoyed a luxurious suite with a crystal chandelier and thick oriental rugs”*
4. produced or growing in extreme abundance
*“their riotous blooming”*

<a id="entry-l-003564"></a>
## luxuriantly

*adv.* 1. **充裕+地, 繁茂+地, 豐富+地** — in an abundant and luxuriant manner
*“whiskers grew luxuriantly from his ample jowls”*
2. **豐富+地, 繁茂+地** — in an elegantly luxuriant way
*“this suave, culture-loving and luxuriantly good-looking M.P.represents the car-workers of Coventry”*

<a id="entry-l-003565"></a>
## luxuriate

2. become extravagant; indulge (oneself) luxuriously
3. thrive profusely or flourish extensively

<a id="entry-l-003566"></a>

**發音：** /lʌɡˈʒʊəɹi.əs/

2. **奢侈+的, 特別精良+的, 奢華+的, 豪華+的, 高級+的** — rich and superior in quality
*“a princely sum”*
*“gilded dining rooms”*
3. displaying luxury and furnishing gratification to the senses
*“an epicurean banquet”*
*“enjoyed a luxurious suite with a crystal chandelier and thick oriental rugs”*

<a id="entry-l-003567"></a>

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **萊姆病 /萊姆病, 萊姆 /萊姆** — Infection by one of three or more subspecies of the bacterium Borrelia burgdorferi, which is transmitted by four species of tick in genus Ixodes. Symptoms include a rash followed by fever, joint pain, and headaches.

<a id="entry-l-003671"></a>

## lymph

**發音：** /lɪmf/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **淋巴** — A colourless, watery, coagulable bodily fluid which bathes the tissues and is carried by the lymphatic system into the bloodstream; it resembles blood plasma in containing white blood cells and especially lymphocytes but normally few red blood cells and no platelets.
2. a thin coagulable fluid (similar to plasma but) containing white blood cells (lymphocytes) and chyle; is conveyed to the blood stream by lymphatic vessels

<a id="entry-l-003673"></a>
## lymph gland

*n.* 1. *(informal)* **淋巴腺** — Synonym of lymph node.
2. the source of lymph and lymphocytes

<a id="entry-l-003674"></a>
## lymph node

*n.* 1. **淋巴結 /淋巴結** — Each of the small oval bodies of the lymphatic system, distributed along the lymphatic vessels, that are clustered in the armpits, groin, neck, chest and abdomen. They act as filters, with an internal honeycomb of connective tissue filled with lymphocytes and macrophages that collect and destroy bacteria, viruses and foreign matter from lymph. When the body is fighting an infection, these lymphocytes multiply rapidly and produce a characteristic swelling of the lymph nodes.
2. the source of lymph and lymphocytes

<a id="entry-l-003675"></a>
## lymph vessel

*n.* 1. **淋巴管** — A thin-walled vessel that carries lymph through the lymphatic system.
2. a vascular duct that carries lymph which is eventually added to the venous blood circulation

<a id="entry-l-003676"></a>
## lymphadenitis

*n.* 1. **淋巴結炎** — inflammation of lymph nodes

<a id="entry-l-003677"></a>
## lymphadenoma

*n.* 1. **惡性淋巴瘤** — an abnormally enlarged lymph node

<a id="entry-l-003678"></a>
## lymphadenopathy

**發音：** /lɪmfædəˈnɒpəθi/
*n.* 1. **淋巴結病 /淋巴結病** — An abnormal enlargement of the lymph nodes; it is often a nonspecific sign of infection but is also often of obscure origin and benign.
2. **淋巴結病** — chronic abnormal enlargement of the lymph nodes (usually associated with disease)

<a id="entry-l-003679"></a>
## lymphangiectasia

*n.* 1. **淋巴管擴張** — dilatation of a lymph vessel

<a id="entry-l-003680"></a>
## lymphangiectasis

*n.* 1. **淋巴管擴張** — dilatation of a lymph vessel

<a id="entry-l-003681"></a>
## lymphangiogram

*n.* 1. **淋巴管造影** — an angiogram of the lymph nodes and lymph vessels made after the injection of a radiopaque substance

<a id="entry-l-003682"></a>
## lymphangioma

*n.* 1. **淋巴管瘤** — benign angioma consisting of a mass of lymphatic vessels

<a id="entry-l-003683"></a>
## lymphangitis

*n.* 1. **淋巴管炎** — inflammation of a lymph vessel

<a id="entry-l-003684"></a>
## lymphatic

*adj.* 1. **含淋巴+的, 淋巴分泌+的, 輸送淋巴+的, 淋巴+的** — of or relating to or produced by lymph

<a id="entry-l-003685"></a>
## lymphatic system

*n.* 1. **淋巴系統 /淋巴系統** — In mammals, including humans, a network of lymph vessels and lymph nodes that transport fluid, fats, proteins, and lymphocytes to the bloodstream as lymph, and remove microorganisms and other debris from tissues.
2. the interconnected system of spaces and vessels between body tissues and organs by which lymph circulates throughout the body

<a id="entry-l-003686"></a>
## lymphedema

*n.* 1. **淋巴水腫 /淋巴水腫, 淋巴水腫** — The swelling of tissue due to retention of fluid in the lymph vessels.
2. **慢性淋巴水腫** — swelling (usually in the legs) caused by lymph accumulating in the tissues in the affected areas

<a id="entry-l-003687"></a>
## lymphoblastic leukemia

*n.* 1. **淋巴細胞性白血病** — a form of lymphocytic leukemia in which the abnormal cells in the circulating blood are almost totally lymphoblasts

<a id="entry-l-003688"></a>
## lymphocyte

*n.* 1. **淋巴細胞 /淋巴細胞** — Any of several types of white blood cell with a spherical nucleus occurring in the lymphatic system, including B cells, T cells and natural killer cells.
2. an agranulocytic leukocyte that normally makes up a quarter of the white blood cell count but increases in the presence of infection

<a id="entry-l-003689"></a>
## lymphocytic

*adj.* 1. **淋巴球+的, 淋巴細胞+的** — of or relating to lymphocytes
*“lymphocytic leukemia”*

<a id="entry-l-003690"></a>
## lymphocytic leukemia

*n.* 1. **慢性淋巴細胞性白血病** — leukemia characterized by enlargement of lymphoid tissues and lymphocytic cells in the circulating blood

<a id="entry-l-003691"></a>
## lymphocytopenia

*n.* 1. **淋巴細胞減少** — an abnormally small number of lymphocytes in the circulating blood

<a id="entry-l-003692"></a>

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **性病性淋巴肉芽腫 /性病性淋巴肉芽腫, 第四性病** — A sexually transmitted disease caused by Chlamydia trachomatis.
2. infectious disease caused by a species of chlamydia bacterium; transmitted by sexual contact; characterized by genital lesions and swelling of lymph nodes in the groin

**發音：** /lɪmˈfoʊ.mə/

2. **淋巴瘤** — a neoplasm of lymph tissue that is usually malignant; one of the four major types of cancer

<a id="entry-l-003695"></a>

## lynch

**發音：** /lɪnt͡ʃ/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **執行私刑 /執行私刑** — To execute (somebody) without a proper legal trial or procedure, especially by hanging and backed by a mob.
2. **加私刑, 處以私刑, 執行私刑** — kill without legal sanction
*“The blood-thirsty mob lynched the alleged killer of the child”*

<a id="entry-l-003699"></a>
## lynch law

*n.* 1. **私刑** — the practice of punishing people by hanging without due process of law

<a id="entry-l-003700"></a>

*n.* 1. **私刑, 私罰 /私罰** — Execution of a person by mob action without due process of law, especially by hanging.
2. putting a person to death by mob action without due process of law

## lyrebird

*n.* 1. **琴鳥 /琴鳥** — Either of two large ground-dwelling Australian songbirds, of the genus Menura, notable for their extraordinary ability to mimic natural and artificial sounds from their environment.
2. Australian bird that resembles a pheasant; the courting male displays long tail feathers in a lyre shape

<a id="entry-l-003718"></a>
## lyric

*n.* 1. the text of a popular song or musical-comedy number
*“his compositions always started with the lyrics”*
*“he wrote both words and music”*
2. a short poem of songlike quality
*adj.* 1. **抒情+的** — relating to or being musical drama
*“the lyric stage”*
2. **抒情+的** — of or relating to a category of poetry that expresses emotion (often in a songlike way)
*“lyric poetry”*
3. expressing deep emotion
*“the dancer's lyrical performance”*
4. used of a singer or singing voice that is light in volume and modest in range
*“a lyric soprano”*
*v.* 1. write lyrics for (a song)

<a id="entry-l-003719"></a>
## lyric poetry

*n.* 1. *(no-gloss, translation-hub)* **抒情詩 /抒情詩**

<a id="entry-l-003720"></a>

<a id="entry-l-003721"></a>
## lyrically

*“she danced the part of the Black Swan very lyrically”*

3. **溶解, 裂解** — The disintegration or destruction of cells.
